### [1] [Nucleation beyond Equilibrium: Fronts Control Invasion in Bistable Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05251)
*Victor Lequin,Giulio Biroli,Camille Scalliet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为具有非守恒向量序参量的反应-扩散系统推导了广义成核理论，证明了经典成核理论（CNT）的数学结构在非平衡条件下依然成立，只需将能量量替换为前沿动力学性质（前沿速度替代体自由能差，扩散系数替代表面张力）。将该理论应用于两物种Lotka-Volterra模型后，发现强种间竞争会在前沿内产生显著的种群亏缺区，使入侵难度随竞争增强而指数级增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典成核理论依赖于细致平衡（微观可逆性）条件，仅在平衡系统中成立。然而生态动力学普遍违反细致平衡，且由多个耦合的非守恒丰度场（向量序参量）描述，缺乏通用的成核理论来定量描述空间扩展系统中的状态入侵过程。

Method: 针对具有非守恒向量序参量的反应-扩散系统，分别推导了紧邻双结线（通过传播前沿入侵）和紧邻旋节线（亚稳态失去稳定性）两种情形的成核理论；利用拟势（quasipotential）的大量数值计算在两种区域对理论进行验证；并将理论应用于两物种Lotka-Volterra模型。

Result: 数值计算证实了理论在双结线和旋节线附近的正确性。理论表明CNT的数学结构在非平衡状态下得以保留，其中前沿速度等效于体自由能差，扩散系数等效于表面张力。在Lotka-Volterra模型中，强种间竞争在前沿内产生显著的亏缺区（总丰度远低于环境容量），该向量结构使入侵难度随竞争增强而指数增加。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典成核理论推广到非平衡、向量序参量的生态系统中，揭示了前沿内部动力学结构对入侵概率的关键影响，并提出了可在微生物系统中实验验证的可检验预测。

Abstract: Bistability, the existence of two alternative stable states with distinct basins of attraction, is common across ecology and many other biological, chemical, and physical systems. In spatially extended systems, the invasion of one state by the other proceeds through nucleation, the fluctuation-driven growth of a droplet beyond a critical size. Classical Nucleation Theory (CNT) quantifies this process using the energy landscape, but this description relies on detailed balance, the condition of microscopic reversibility that holds at equilibrium. Ecological dynamics generally violate detailed balance and are described not by a single scalar field but by several coupled, non-conserved abundances--a vector order parameter--for which no general nucleation theory exists. Here we derive such a theory for reaction-diffusion systems with a non-conserved vector order parameter, valid both close to the binodal, where invasion proceeds through propagating fronts, and close to the spinodal, where the metastable state loses stability. Extensive numerical computations of the quasipotential confirm the theory in both regimes. We show that the mathematical structure of CNT survives out of equilibrium, once energetic quantities are replaced by dynamical properties of fronts: the front speed plays the role of the bulk free-energy difference between phases, and the diffusivity that of the surface tension. Applied to the two-species Lotka-Volterra model, an archetypal system of bistable ecological antagonism, our theory shows that strong interspecific competition generates a pronounced depletion region within fronts, where the total abundance falls well below carrying capacity. This vectorial structure, invisible to a scalar description based on species frequency alone, makes invasion exponentially harder as competition strengthens at fixed competitive advantage--a prediction testable in microbial systems.

</details>


### [2] [Superfluid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05354)
*Charles Radin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个数学上可解的玩具模型，用于模拟超流氦-4（原子序数为4的氦同位素）从正常液相到超流相的相变，并展示了如何为该超流体获取一个序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立超流氦-4相变的可解模型，从而能够理解并提取超流体的序参量。

Method: 提出了一个数学上可解的玩具模型，用来刻画正常液体与超流体之间的相变，并利用该模型推导序参量。

Result: 该模型能够展示如何为超流体获得一个序参量。

Conclusion: 通过可解的玩具模型，可以阐明超流氦-4相变机制并获得相应的序参量。

Abstract: We are interested in modelling superfluid helium-4, the common isotope of helium, of atomic number 4. We present a mathematically solvable toy model of the phase transition between the normal liquid and superfluid phases and use it to show how an order parameter might be obtained for the superfluid.

</details>


### [3] [Theory of Measurement-Altered Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05289)
*Kabir Khanna,Sara Murciano,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出弱测量Tomonaga-Luttinger液体的理论，通过replica instanton计算发现测量诱导的随机性使平均关联函数呈现带对数修正的普适幂律衰减，并揭示了后测量态系综中关联矩的宽泛多分形谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部测量可以改变无能隙量子物质中的长程关联，但测量记录的固有随机性使得理解测量如何改变临界量子态成为挑战。作者希望建立一个超越强制测量和常规临界标度理论的框架，来刻画测量对1+1维量子临界态的影响。

Method: 1. 提出弱测量Tomonaga-Luttinger液体的理论框架；2. 发展replica instanton计算方法研究Born平均的可观测量；3. 分析密度和相位涨落关联函数的长程行为；4. 表征关联函数矩的多分形谱；5. 使用矩阵乘积态（MPS）数值计算验证解析结果。

Result: 当测量为相关（relevant）时，平均关联函数在长距离以带对数修正的普适幂律衰减，这一特征被认为是测量诱导随机性所特有的。完整的多分形谱揭示了后测量态系综中广泛且强非高斯的涨落。MPS计算支持了解析结果。

Conclusion: 物理测量以一种既超越强制测量又超越常规临界标度的方式改变临界量子态。该工作为1+1维共形场论描述的基态提供了测量改变临界性的普适图景。

Abstract: Local measurements can alter long-range correlations in gapless quantum matter. We propose a theory of weakly-monitored Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids, a broad class of quantum critical states in one dimension. In order to address the intrinsic randomness of the measurement record, we develop a replica instanton calculation to study Born-averaged observables. We find that when measurements are relevant, average correlators of density and phase fluctuations decay at long distances as universal power laws with logarithmic corrections, a feature we argue is peculiar to measurement-induced randomness. We characterize the full multifractal spectrum of moments of correlations functions, revealing broad, strongly non-gaussian fluctuations across the ensemble of post-measurement states. We support these analytic results with matrix-product-state calculations, and provide a general picture of measurement-altered criticality for ground states described by 1+1d conformal field theories. Our results establish that physical measurements alter critical quantum states in a manner that lies beyond both forced measurements and conventional critical scaling.

</details>


### [4] [Morphology of frozen labyrinths from irreversible threshold dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05496)
*Daniel Richard Levy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究不可逆（单次翻转）多数阈值动力学在格点上的演化行为，发现其从随机初始条件会冻结为不平衡的非共识迷宫图案，该图案是停滞粗化的双连续结构，其大规模布局由初始条件确定性决定，更新顺序仅起次要调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数阈值动力学（MBO类型）是可精确求解的模型，其可逆形式使界面按平均曲率运动。但当规则变为不可逆（每个代理最多翻转一次）时，系统行为发生根本改变，这一不可逆变体的演化机制和图案形成规律此前未被系统理解。

Method: 使用高斯核在格点上建模代理间相互作用，比较可逆（MBO阈值动力学）与不可逆（单次翻转）规则的差异。通过操控实验确定机制：固定初始条件同时改变更新顺序，分离确定性部分与随机部分；并分析冻结墙（frustration）的几何特征作为机制标识。

Result: 不可逆单次翻转规则从随机初始条件冻结出平衡的非共识迷宫图案，可逆弛豫则会将系统驱向共识或条纹。图案存在窗口：初始自旋组成接近平衡时出现，窗口宽度随相互作用范围增大而收窄。冻结形态是停滞粗化，特征宽度亚线性增长。改变更新顺序仅影响墙壁位置的微调，大规模布局是确定性的。冻结墙表现为代理被冻结在自身局部场对面，这一特征在可逆弛豫中完全不存在，且几乎不携带图案的大尺度形状信息。

Conclusion: 不可逆多数阈值动力学产生一种由确定性初始条件主导、更新顺序仅起次要作用的冻结迷宫图案。其机制可通过"挫败"（frustration）标识唯一识别，该研究为理解不可逆动力学中的停滞粗化和图案冻结提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Majority threshold dynamics, in which each agent adopts the dominant state in a weighted neighborhood, relaxes a binary field toward consensus or stripes. We study what happens when this rule is made irreversible: each agent, interacting through a Gaussian kernel on a lattice, may flip out of its local weighted minority at most once. The reversible form is threshold dynamics of Merriman-Bence-Osher type, an exactly solvable calibration in which interfaces move by mean curvature with closed-form lattice pinning and mobility. Irreversibility changes the outcome. From random initial conditions, the one-flip rule freezes balanced non-consensus labyrinths that reversible relaxation drives away. The patterned regime is a window of initial spin compositions around equal balance, narrowing as the interaction range grows, controlled by a standardized bias whose onset is independent of scale over a fourfold range. The frozen morphology is arrested coarsening: bicontinuous at balance, with a feature width that grows sublinearly and falls below the interaction range at large scales. We determine the mechanism by intervention. Fixing the initial condition while varying the update order shows that the coarse domain layout is deterministic, while the update order enters only in a secondary first-passage race that fine-tunes the wall positions the deterministic dynamics has already set. Those walls are marked by frustration: agents frozen against their own local field. This signature is identically absent from any reversible relaxation, is overwhelmingly interfacial, and carries almost none of the pattern's large-scale shape.

</details>


### [5] [Entanglement Scaling and Full Counting Statistics in Excited States of Two-Dimensional Rotating Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05722)
*Priyangshu Goswami,Abhishek Dhar,Satya N. Majumdar,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维旋转谐振子阱中N粒子费米子激发态的纠缠熵。解析计算了圆盘区域内Rényi熵和粒子数涨落累积量，发现纠缠熵的面积律缩放同样适用于激发态，并验证了非相互作用费米子系统纠缠熵的粒子数累积量级数展开式，同时推导了居中累积量生成函数，证明了概率分布与基态具有相同的缩放性质，最后将结果推广到环形区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵在量子多体系统研究中具有重要意义，大多数工作聚焦于基态性质，而对激发态中纠缠熵的研究相对较少。本文旨在探讨二维旋转费米子系统中激发态的纠缠熵及其面积律缩放是否仍然成立，以及激发态与基态在熵和粒子数涨落特性上的联系。

Method: 构造由填充特定N个单粒子能级组成的二维修正谐振子阱中旋转费米子的激发态。通过在阱中心周围半径为r的圆盘内解析计算q阶Rényi熵和粒子数涨落累积量，对非相互作用费米子系统验证了纠缠熵的粒子数累积量级数展开，推导居中累积量生成函数，并将结果扩展到环形区域。

Result: 发现纠缠熵的面积律缩放对一类激发态仍然成立；验证了非相互作用费米子纠缠熵的粒子数累积量级数展开；推导出居中累积量生成函数，证明了圆盘内概率分布函数与已知基态结果具有相同的缩放性质（仅差一个变量平移）；环形区域的Rényi熵和粒子数累积量可分解为两个边界圆盘对应量之和。

Conclusion: 激发态中的纠缠熵仍然遵循面积律缩放。环形区域的可加性分解关系在环宽足够大的条件下成立，揭示了旋转费米子系统中激发态与基态在熵性质上的内在关联。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of a class of $N$-particle excited state of fermions confined in a two-dimensional harmonic trap rotating at an angular frequency $Ω$. The excited state is constructed by filling a particular set of $N$ single-particle energy levels. We analytically compute the Rényi entropies of order $q$ in a disc of radius $r$ around the centre of the trap, and the cumulants corresponding to number fluctuations of fermions within the disc. We found that the area law scaling of entanglement entropy holds even for a class of excited states. We also verified the well-known series expansion of entanglement entropy in terms of the particle number cumulants for non-interacting fermions. We further derive the centered cumulant generating function, demonstrating that the associated probability distribution function in the disc has identical scaling properties, up to a variable shift, to the known ground-state result. Finally, we extend our result to an annular region, showing that both the Rényi entropy and particle number cumulants of the annulus decompose into sums of the corresponding quantities of the two bounding discs. These additive relations hold as long as the width of the annulus is sufficiently large.

</details>


### [6] [Computing Shear Viscosities from Molecular Dynamics Simulation: Comparing the OrthoBoXY Approach with the Green-Kubo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05845)
*Marcel Brandt,Ralf Ludwig,Dietmar Paschek*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过平衡分子动力学（MD）模拟，使用OrthoBoXY方法计算了15种纯分子液体的剪切粘度，并与Green-Kubo方法的结果进行了验证对比。研究发现粘度不受有限尺寸效应影响（系统小至250个分子），且标准误差几乎与系统大小无关。作者提出应优先运行较小系统、更长模拟时间，并改进了OrthoBoXY方法的模块长度（τ_block）配方，对于高粘性系统可缩减至1/8，低粘性系统可缩减至1/4，高流动性系统不宜缩减，从而在保持精度的同时降低最多24倍的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切粘度是分子液体性质模拟中的重要物理量，OrthoBoXY方法虽已提出但缺乏系统的参数优化和误差分析。作者希望通过与Green-Kubo方法的系统对比，揭示OrthoBoXY方法的最佳实践，特别是系统尺寸、模拟时长和模块长度等关键参数的选取策略，以最大化计算效率而不牺牲数值精度。

Method: 采用平衡分子动力学（MD）模拟，对15种纯分子液体使用OrthoBoXY方法计算剪切粘度，并以Green-Kubo方法作为基准验证。进行了多系统尺寸（最小250个分子）和多运行时长的模拟，系统考察有限尺寸效应对粘度及标准误差的影响。还基于轨迹数据的统计分析，对OrthoBoXY模块长度τ_block配方进行细化修正。

Result: 1) OrthoBoXY与Green-Kubo两种方法的结果高度一致；2) 粘度不受有限尺寸效应影响，系统可小至250个分子；3) 粘度标准误差几乎与系统尺寸无关，这是自扩散系数精度随尺寸增大而提高与OrthoBoXY公式中尺寸相关权重相互补偿的结果；4) 提出了τ_block的改进配方：高粘性系统可缩减1/8，低粘性系统可缩减1/4，高流动性系统不建议缩减；5) 小型系统（250分子）结合修正后的配方，可最多降低24倍计算成本而不损失精度。

Conclusion: 作者建议在实际计算中优先使用较小系统（约250分子）配合更长的模拟时间，并依据系统粘性程度选择相应的τ_block缩放因子。这一策略在保持数值精度的前提下显著降低计算资源消耗，为OrthoBoXY方法的实用化提供了明确的参数指导。

Abstract: We calculated shear viscosities of 15 neat molecular liquids from equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) simulations using the OrthoBoXY approach and compare them to viscosities calculated via the Green-Kubo method. Data from both methods agree very well. Here, we show how to avoid pitfalls while computing the OrthoBoXY-data to obtain optimal results. From simulations of multiple system sizes, we could verify that the viscosity of molecular liquids is not influenced by finite size effects down to systems as small as 250 molecules. Moreover, we demonstrate that also the standard error of the viscosity is nearly independent of the system size. This is shown to be a consequence of a compensation effect of an increasing accuracy of the self-diffusion coefficients with increasing systems-size and the system-size dependent weighting according to the OrthoBoXY-equation. As a consequence, we suggest that it is preferable to run simulations of smaller systems with longer simulation times rather than larger systems with shorter simulation runs. In addition, we discuss a refinement of the recently introduced "recipe" for OrthoBoXY simulations block-lengths $τ_\mathrm{block}$. Based on data from simulations with varying run-lengths, we suggest the following modification: for highly viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/8$, significantly reducing the computational resources needed. For less viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/4$. For systems with high fluidity, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ should not be scaled down in order to achieve reliable results. When using a smaller system size of 250 molecules, these refinements are leading up to a 24-fold reduction in computational cost compared to the previous recommended set-up without sacrificing numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [7] [Dynamic scaling behavior in the presence of a periodic magnetic driving across Ising continuous transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05936)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维伊辛模型在临界温度下受周期性磁场驱动时的临界动力学行为，发现了由时间、振幅和周期三者相互作用产生的奇异动态标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解周期性外场驱动下临界系统的动力学行为，探索时间依赖周期源与序参量场耦合如何影响连续相变过程中的临界动力学。

Method: 采用纯弛豫动力学的二维伊辛模型，施加周期性磁场h(t)=-A cos(2πt/P)，在临界温度下通过数值模拟研究其热力学极限下的动态标度行为，引入标度变量τ=t/P和σ=A P^κ（κ=y_h/z≈0.865）。

Result: 发现周期性驱动产生独特的动态标度行为，磁化和键能密度的动态标度呈现围绕光滑曲线的振荡行为，该曲线在τ较大时趋近于一个稳态行为。

Conclusion: 周期性时变外场驱动的临界系统展现出非平凡的动态标度行为，标度变量由场振幅、周期和临界指数共同决定；同时文中也简要讨论了温度周期性变化驱动的类似现象。

Abstract: We study the critical dynamics arising from a time-dependent periodic homogenous source coupled to the order-parameter field, which drives a classical ferromagnetic system across a continuous transition. For this purpose, we consider the paradigmatic two-dimensional (2D) Ising model in the presence of a periodic magnetic field $h(t)=-A\, \cos (2πt/P)$, evolving under a purely relaxational dynamics at the critical temperature. We show that the periodic driving gives rise to a peculiar dynamic scaling behavior in the thermodynamic limit, arising from a nontrivial interplay among the time $t$, the amplitude $A$ and period $P$ of $h(t)$. The relevant scaling variables are $τ=t/P$ and $σ=A P^κ$, with $κ= y_h/z$, where $y_h=(d+2-η)/2$ is the critical dimension of the magnetic field, and $z$ is dynamic exponent for the critical relaxational dynamics ($κ\approx 0.865$ for the 2D Ising model). The dynamic scaling behaviors of the magnetization and bond-energy density show an oscillatory behavior around a smooth curve which approaches a large-$τ$ stationary behavior. We also briefly discuss the dynamic behavior of an Ising system driven across the critical point by a periodic time-varying temperature at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [8] [Measurement-induced entanglement Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06006)
*Viktor Eisler,Erik Tonni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了无穷跳跃链在基态下、对占据数基进行部分投影测量后的纠缠哈密顿量。对于被两个测量区域与链其余部分隔开的段，证明了约化密度矩阵可通过共形映射和规范变换与巨正则态相关联。纠缠哈密顿量由局部逆温度描述，该温度在端点附近以平方根形式消失，且与具体测量结果无关。但局部化学势与段内诱导电荷密度相关，因此后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量比相应的熵包含更多关于测量结果的信息。

Result: Tc(α)呈非单调关系，在中等持久性处存在最小值，且该最小值深度随R增大而减小。捕获动力学表明Tc由捕获过程的极端晚时尾部主导，而大部分目标在更早时间被捕获；该尾部主要由自由探索阶段而非最终定向接近阶段控制。分配策略方面，单轮与级联策略在极小R下一致，而最大基数匹配在中等R下已产生显著加速：大R下Tc降低数倍，中等R下降低超过一个数量级。

Abstract: We study a minimal lattice model of active search-and-capture in which persistent random walkers locate and irreversibly capture immobile targets through a finite-range, mutually exclusive assignment rule. We measure the collective completion time $T_c$ as a function of the walkers' reorientation rate $α$ and the search radius $R$. The dependence $T_c(α)$ is non-monotonic, with a minimum at intermediate persistence whose depth decreases as $R$ grows. Capture kinetics show that $T_c$ is not a typical capture time but is governed by the extreme, late-time tail of the capture process, while the bulk of targets are captured much earlier; this tail is controlled mainly by the free-exploration phase rather than by the final directed approach. We then compare the baseline single-round assignment rule with cascading reassignment and with maximum-cardinality matching on a candidate graph. The two greedy policies (single-round and cascading) agree at very small $R$, whereas maximum-cardinality matching already produces a strong speedup at moderate $R$: improved matching reduces $T_c$ by factors of several at large $R$, and by more than an order of magnitude at moderate $R$. Thus, in this collective, depletion-coupled search problem, the assignment policy can control the capture time more strongly than the walkers' persistence.

</details>

Abstract: A thermodynamic theory for transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids is developed from a general expression for the Onsager matrix. The theory predicts the ratio $σ/σ_{\rm id}$, where $σ$ is a transport coefficient and $σ_{\rm id}$ denotes its dilute-gas value. This ratio depends exclusively on equilibrium thermodynamic properties and can therefore be computed directly from the equation of state. Compact expressions are obtained for the thermal conductivity, viscosity, and self-diffusion coefficient. These expressions quantitatively reproduce simulation data over almost the entire fluid range and, in the case of self-diffusion, significantly improve upon the predictions of Enskog kinetic theory. The results demonstrate that transport coefficients can be accurately described within a purely thermodynamic framework.

</details>

Result: 发现两个定律：(1) 任意速率变化对平均首次通过时间的对数灵敏度绝对值不超过1；(2) 所有速率的灵敏度之和为-1。这些结果意味着通过某些跃迁加速完成必须以减慢其他跃迁为代价，且协同改变多个速率也无法使完成时间移动超过单个速率所能达到的范围。该预算还限定了动力学校验区分度以上界为检查点数目，并给出了对底物浓度的灵敏度代价。

Abstract: The first-passage time is the natural observable of reaction completion, yet how its mean responds to a rate change has lacked a general constraint. We show that the logarithmic sensitivity of the mean first-passage time of any finite Markov chain to any rate is bounded by one in magnitude, and that these sensitivities sum to -1. Together the two laws form a conserved control budget: speeding completion through some transitions must be paid for by others, and a coordinated change shifts the completion time only as far as the budget allows. Raising an activation barrier or shifting the depth of a well moves many rates at once, yet neither can shift the completion time further than a single rate could. The budget caps kinetic-proofreading discrimination at the checkpoint count, and prices it in sensitivity to substrate concentration.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用成分数据分析（Compositional Data Analysis）从超过1000名儿童的队列数据中构建时间使用组合与多种健康指标（如BMI、生活满意度、认知）之间的目标函数；提出一种新的不确定性感知QD（Quality Diversity）方法，将预测不确定性嵌入QD优化过程；通过基于变量和基于目标的行为表征探索解空间，揭示多样化的高质量时间使用组合及健康结果间的关系。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，该框架为行为健康中的时间使用推荐提供了更可靠的决策支持，克服了传统仅关注最大预期收益的局限性。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>

*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计随机试验和观察性研究中结局排序期望（DOOR）概率，以评估获益-风险。该框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函，通过序列风险集危险度估计条件有序分布，并推导出DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。模拟结果表明TMLE-SL在点估计上表现最优，CVTMLE-SL在推断性能上整体最佳。方法通过抗菌耐药领导力小组的多重耐药菌网络数据得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机试验和观察性研究中，获益-风险评估需要综合多种结局（如疗效和安全性），DOOR概率是常用的评估指标。然而，协变量调整下的DOOR概率估计缺乏统一的因果推断框架，且已有方法在点估计和推断性能上未得到系统性比较，需要发展具有理论保障（如有效影响函数）且实际表现稳健的估计方法。

Method: 将DOOR概率表示为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函；通过顺序风险集危险度（sequential risk-set hazards）估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。在点估计部分比较了G-computation、归一化逆概率加权（IPW）、增广IPW（AIPW）和目标最大似然估计（TMLE），并使用广义线性模型或Super Learner（SL）估计干扰函数；在推断部分评估了AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL（有无交叉拟合，即CVTMLE-SL）的性能。

Result: TMLE-SL在点估计中表现最强且最稳定，AIPW-SL位居第二；在EIF推断评估中，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、底层有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最优。方法在抗菌耐药领导力集团多重耐药菌网络数据上得到应用验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的协变量校正DOOR概率估计框架具有完善的理论性质（双线性泛函表达和EIF推导），结合Super Learner和交叉拟合的TMLE（即CVTMLE-SL）在点估计和推断上均表现出最优的整体性能，为随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评估提供了可靠且可复现的因果推断工具。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>

Method: 提出三种处理缺失值的策略：（1）均匀概率方法——对缺失值赋予均匀分布的概率权重；（2）部分观测方法——仅利用可观测的信息进行分裂分配；（3）降维平滑方法——通过降维后的平滑处理利用部分信息。这些修改被设计为在任意缺失模式（MCAR/MAR/任意模式）下保持概率守恒和边际兼容性等概率性质。在多个具有不同缺失程度的真实数据集上与经典CART进行比较评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，缺失观测的处理方式（fill策略）对预测性能的影响最为显著，甚至超过平滑分布和代理选择准则的影响。在包含大量缺失预测变量的数据集中，所提出的方法经常优于CART，同时保持了树模型的解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 概率回归树能够直接在树构建过程中有效处理缺失预测值，无需预先插补。缺失处理策略的选择（尤其是fill策略）是影响性能的主导因素。在缺失率较高的场景下，这些方法相比经典CART具有明显优势，同时保留了基于树模型的优点。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）提出有限样本理论保证，在Hölder正则性等假设下，证明了局部覆盖间隙和相对oracle长度误差的高概率界，并分析了学习得分情形下的理论分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供有限的边际覆盖保证，但边际有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定错误校准，而精确的无分布条件覆盖在有限样本下无法实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缓解这一差距，但现有理论缺乏对已实现局部化集合的有限样本保证，无法同时控制条件有效性和oracle效率。

Result: 获得了均匀覆盖已实现局部化邻域的条件覆盖间隙和oracle效率（长度误差）的高概率上界；边界分解揭示了带宽h的偏差-方差权衡（局部偏差O(h^β)与校准项）；对于学习得分，证明了改进得分估计（更接近pivotal得分）能锐化局部化保证。

Conclusion: RLCP在适当的正则性假设下能同时实现条件有效性和oracle效率的有限样本保证，且理论结果揭示了带宽选择与得分学习质量对局部化覆盖性能的影响规律。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [18] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中逻辑损失的梯度下降（GD）的隐式偏差问题，证明在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，适当的早期停止可以在协方差谱快速连续衰减（包括多项式与指数衰减）时达到极小极大最优的零一风险，并通过新校准结果获得最优收敛速率；同时证明线性插值器所需的样本量可比早期停止指数级更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化分类中，训练数据可分但底层分布未必可分，GD在逻辑损失下范数发散但方向收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差在统计上可能并非最优。需要寻找能克服这种次优性的机制。

Method: 在含标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，分别推导早期停止迭代的尖锐上界与任意分类器的匹配统计下界；核心创新是一个新的校准定理，将过度逻辑风险转化为零一风险，解决了标签翻转噪声引起的模型误设定问题，并消除了标准界中的平方根速率。

Result: 早期停止在多项式及指数谱衰减下达到极小极大最优的零一风险率；线性插值器要达到相同超额风险所需样本量可能比早期停止多指数级。

Conclusion: 早期停止能从统计上显著优于插值，为过参数化分类中的隐式偏差与泛化提供新的理论理解。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出强化学习框架，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义可控马尔可夫过程；建立马尔可夫链不可约、非周期和几何遍历的充分条件；设计涵盖分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计与结构保持压缩的多目标奖励函数。

Conclusion: 该工作将PD从静态对象拓展为可演化的随机过程，为自适应拓扑简化与概率建模提供了统一框架，为持续图空间的统计推断开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [20] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性插入样本下的最坏情况学习误差。研究表明，当VC维d≥2时，额外添加正确标注样本会使最小最大期望误差从PAC学习的Θ(d/n)恶化到Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，这一对数因子的代价是内在的而非特定算法的伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人提出了一个学习模型：单调对抗者观察干净样本后插入有限个正确标注的额外样本，破坏样本的可交换性。他们发现许多已知最优学习器在该模型下偏离最优的Θ(d/n)速率，并提出疑问：额外对数因子是否只是特定算法的伪影，还是缺乏可交换性的内在后果？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 本文通过构造一个统一的显式类和先验来建立所有下界：在该构造中，两个在非零质量点上不同的目标假设会产生相同的样本，从而使得学习器无法区分。上界方面，对于VC维为1的情形，采用一个简单的非正确学习器，其分析借鉴了一包含图（one-inclusion graph）的留一参数论证。

Result: 在最坏情况下（对VC维d的类和已知有限插入预算），最小最大期望误差在d=1时为Θ(1/n)，在d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。同样的速率在Littlestone维d_L替换d后同样成立，说明干净的在线到批量（online-to-batch）速率O(d_L/n)也是不可达的。

Conclusion: 添加正确标注的样本实际上可以使学习在VC维大于1时变得困难一个对数因子，即使对于具有有限在线错误界的类也是如此。这一结果解决了Larsen等人提出的开放问题，证明额外对数代价是缺乏可交换性的内在后果。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [21] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种克服VARMA模型计算瓶颈的估计框架，通过部分自相关重参数化保证平稳性与可逆性，利用Parseval恒等式将损失函数转化为仅依赖固定大小充分统计量的形式，使得每次优化迭代的计算成本与序列长度T无关。该框架在固定维度下以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，并能在d=10到d=40的维度下保持接近Oracle预测误差，在多个实际数据集上匹配或超越各类基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型虽然能用少量参数捕捉移动平均项，但长期被认为在高维下不可行，原因是：似然函数非凸、参数化仅在等价类下可识别、每次评估都需要遍历整个序列。这导致实践中研究人员不得不依赖纯VAR模型。本文旨在消除VARMA模型的计算障碍，使其适用于中等维度问题。

Method: 1) 采用部分自相关（partial-autocorrelation）重参数化，构造性地保证平稳性和可逆性；2) 在重参数化系数上施加高斯先验，对角元素与非对角元素分别使用不同尺度；3) 利用Parseval（Fourier）恒等式，使损失函数仅依赖固定大小的充分统计量，以截断长度的近线性成本计算；4) 提出两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘估计和协方差边缘化的最大后验（MAP）估计。该框架可推广到季节性动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 理论上证明两个估计器都能在固定维度下以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。实验上，在d=10到d=40维度下估计器接近Oracle预测误差（而经典条件MLE在此范围返回不可逆拟合导致预测发散），在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、逐分量ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于似然的VARMA估计带到实践中依赖VAR模型的规模，每次迭代成本与序列长度无关，从而消除了VARMA在中等维度下长期存在的计算障碍。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>

### [22] [On the motivic cohomology of some singular rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05220)
*Tess Bouis*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用非$\mathbb{A}^1$-不变的回合成上同调，通过积分$p$-进Hodge理论替代传统迹方法，证明了若干奇异环代数$K$-理论计算的回合成细化版本，包括$\mathbb{Z}/p^n$、$\mathbb{Z}[x]/(x^e)$以及连续函数环$\mathscr{C}(X;\mathbb{C})$等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，奇异环的代数$K$-理论计算通常依赖于迹方法（如循环同调、拓扑Hochschild同调等）。作者希望探索一种新的途径，利用积分$p$-进Hodge理论作为迹方法的替代品，以获得这些$K$-理论计算在回合成上同调层面的细化结果，从而揭示更深层的结构信息。

Method: 作者采用非$\mathbb{A}^1$-不变的回合成上同调作为工具，引入积分$p$-进Hodge理论来替代标准迹方法。通过对$\mathbb{Z}/p^n$、$\mathbb{Z}[x]/(x^e)$和$\mathscr{C}(X;\mathbb{C})$（$X$为紧Hausdorff空间）等奇异环的代数$K$-理论进行回合成层面的研究，建立已知$K$-理论计算的细化版本。

Abstract: We show that the weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in every dimension $n\geq14$. Applied to the recent counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, this gives a Hurwitz vector field of degree seven on $\R^{14}$ with three rational singularities. A rank-reduced dehomogenization of the associated cubic stabilization gives, independently, a Hurwitz field of degree three on $\R^{18}$. The dimensions $3\leq n\leq13$ remain open.

Abstract: We study the enumerative geometry of elliptic curves in toric threefolds. Abstract: In this work, we present two novel strategies to impose high-order boundary conditions on fixed and moving curved domains, approximated with piecewise affine triangulations. Achieving high-order accuracy on curved domains requires tackling both the PDE discretization error and the geometrical error simultaneously. While the former can be reduced by employing high-order numerical methods such as finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin, the latter demands either a high-order parametrization of the physical domain or a consistent approximation of the boundary conditions. Minimization-based approaches like the Reconstruction for Off-site Data (ROD) method allow one to skip the construction of high-order curvilinear meshes by defining high-order consistent boundary conditions on a computational boundary that does not match the physical one. The ROD approach mitigates the second-order geometrical error by retrieving a modified polynomial in each boundary cell, which enforces the boundary conditions exactly on the physical boundary. However, the standard ROD method requires the inversion of a local linear system, whose cost grows with the polynomial degree and mesh refinement. Several numerical experiments with Dirichlet and slip-wall boundary conditions are presented, with convergence analysis up to fifth order in both 2D and 3D.

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 对Westervelt方程引入焓变量和原始迁移率变量，形成截断一阶系统，使每个非线性时间步化为一致单调椭圆问题；(2) 利用Browder-Minty定理证明每个时间步子问题的适定性；(3) 推导离散能量不等式保证稳定性；(4) 采用Alt-Luckhaus型紧性论证处理变换变量的弱极限；(5) 建立弱-强唯一性原理和条件性a posteriori判据以确保截断非激活；(6) 对光滑解建立一致性估计，量化Rothe逼近产生的时域残差在自然离散弱范数下的行为。最后通过数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 文章获得了以下结果：(1) Rothe格式的良定义性与适定性（每个时间步的解存在且唯一）；(2) 截断一阶Westervelt系统弱解的存在性证明；(3) 弱-强唯一性原理；(4) 在截断非激活条件下的条件性a posteriori判据；(5) 对充分正则解的一致性估计，确定了Rothe逼近在自然离散时间弱范数中的时间残差，为观测到的时间行为提供了严格的一致性基础；(6) 数值实验展示了格式的稳定性、接近一阶的时间收敛行为以及截断在所测试范围内沿离散轨迹保持非激活。

Conclusion: 本文通过变量变换策略成功克服了Westervelt方程全隐式时间离散化的技术困难，提供了一套完整的理论框架——从适定性、弱解存在性到一致性与唯一性——并辅以数值验证。该方法避免了对高阶能量估计和反不等式的依赖，为非线性声学中此类问题的数值求解提供了新的严格基础。未来工作可能包括将该框架推广到更一般的非线性声学模型或高阶时间离散格式。

Abstract: We study a fully implicit Rothe time discretization for a cutoff first-order formulation of the Westervelt equation. The key ingredients are the enthalpy variable and the primitive mobility variable, which turn each nonlinear time step into a uniformly monotone elliptic problem and avoid higher-order energy estimates and inverse inequalities. For every time step, the discrete problem reduces to a monotone elliptic equation, which yields well-posedness of the Rothe scheme by the Browder-Minty theorem. We derive a discrete energy inequality, establish compactness for the transformed variable by an Alt-Luckhaus type argument, and pass to the limit to obtain existence of weak solutions for the cutoff first-order system. We prove a weak-strong uniqueness principle for the cutoff problem and formulate a conditional a posteriori criterion under which the cutoff is inactive. For sufficiently regular solutions of the forced cutoff problem, we establish consistency estimates which identify the temporal residuals produced by the Rothe approximation in the natural discrete-in-time weak norms. These estimates provide a rigorous consistency basis for the observed temporal behavior. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the stability of the scheme, its observed near first-order temporal behavior, and inactivity of the cutoff along the computed discrete trajectories in the tested regimes.

</details>

Abstract: Mechanistic chromatography models are valuable for process development, but gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization can be hindered by computational cost and the effort of deriving objective-function gradients. Two high-order spatial discretizations, finite difference summation-by-parts (FD-SBP) and the discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method (DG-SEM), are compared on a 6-component ion-exchange chromatography problem with steric mass action kinetics. Both attain their theoretical convergence rates on manufactured problems, while FD-SBP shows favourable work-precision performance for both forward (primal) and dual-valued simulations and is conceptually simpler to implement. DFSA scales as tDFSA approx 1.4(1+Np)tfwd for up to Np = 24 parameters propagated in a single ForwardDiff.jl chunk, i.e. about 1.4 forward solves per additional parameter. Because the primal and dual-valued solves share the same code path, DFSA provides accurate gradients of user-defined objectives without any hand-derived chain-rule code, enabling accessible gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization.

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimising an energy over an unknown manifold that is given implicitly by a point cloud. For a known manifold one can define a gradient descent scheme analogously to the classical construction in Euclidean spaces. However, when the manifold is not known one has to simultaneously estimate the manifold whilst minimising the energy. We define a gradient descent scheme which remains in a neighbourhood of the manifold and, under suitable stability and sampling assumptions, converges to a neighbourhood of a local minimiser whose size vanishes as the time step and sampling errors vanish. As an example we show the application of the methodology to learning operator corrections in inverse problems.

Abstract: Accurate prediction of tritium behaviour in molten salt breeding blankets is essential for the design and safe operation of ARC-class fusion reactors. This work presents a fully open-source, component-scale multiphysics framework for modelling tritium transport in an ARC liquid immersion blanket. Neutron transport, thermal hydraulics, and hydrogen isotope transport are coupled using OpenMC, OpenFOAM, and FESTIM, leveraging dedicated tools enabling direct transfer of spatially resolved fields between solvers. Assuming a zero inlet concentration, steady-state simulations predict a total tritium inventory of approximately 243 mg, with the blanket reaching steady-state tritium throughput within approximately 30 min, which is of a similar order to previous system-level estimates. The results show that tritium transport is dominated by turbulence-enhanced diffusion, with strong localisation in flow stagnation regions and reduced accumulation in highly turbulent zones. Sensitivity analyses indicate that predicted inventories are governed primarily by the numerical stabilisation scheme, with only a modest dependence on the turbulent Schmidt number. The proposed workflow provides a transparent and extensible basis for high-fidelity analysis of tritium transport in ARC-class breeding blankets.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TAR（TAkens Reconstruction）的算法框架，通过整合Takens延迟嵌入定理、流形学习技术和通用函数逼近器，从低维时间序列中分析和重建任意动力系统。该框架在多种模拟和真实动力系统中得到验证，包括生态学捕食者-猎物系统、蛋白质Villin的分子动力学模拟以及Vanguard S&P 500交易记录，并发布了开源软件包。

Result: TAR在多个系统中验证有效：在生态系统中，简单捕食者-猎物动力学可在广泛的嵌入时间尺度下成功重建；在Villin蛋白质分子动力学模拟中，通过同一观测序列的多个时间延迟改进了多特征时间尺度系统的重建效果；在Vanguard S&P 500交易记录中，该方法揭示了潜在动力学现象并在短时间范围内实现了准确的收益预测。

Conclusion: TAR提供了一个通用、可操作的算法框架，将理论嵌入定理转化为实用的重建工具，可应用于任意动力系统，并通过开源软件包降低了使用门槛。

Abstract: Embedding theorems can be used to provide theoretical guarantees about the relation between low-dimensional observations of a system and its full-dimensional state and dynamics. Such theorems do not, however, provide guidance on observable choice, embedding construction, or methodologies to learn the mapping between the embedding and full-dimensional state. In this work, we develop an algorithmic framework, TAkens Reconstruction (TAR), to analyze and reconstruct arbitrary dynamical systems from low-dimensional time series using an integration of Takens' Delay Embedding Theorem, manifold learning techniques, and universal function approximators. We validate TAR in applications to a variety of simulated and observed dynamical systems and use it to investigate how delay vector structure impacts reconstruction accuracy. In an ecological system, we show that simple predator-prey dynamics can be reconstructed with observations taken over a wide variety of embedding time scales. In molecular dynamics simulations of the protein Villin, we demonstrate how including multiple time delays of the same observable series can be used to improve reconstruction of systems with multiple characteristic time scales. In the trade record of Vanguard S&P 500, we show how the approach exposes underlying dynamical phenomenologies in the data and accurate return predictions over short time horizons without access to full-dimensional market observations. We develop and release an open-source software package to enable the application of TAR to arbitrary dynamical systems.

</details>

Result: 在最终基准总结中，梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）以MS研究评分93.83排名第一，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异度0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个针对GSE17048全血RNA数据中MS与健康对照分类的开源基准，提供了可复现的评估流程和外部模型提交机制。该评分仅供研究比较使用，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [53] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTER是一种针对冻结预训练预测器的残差校正方法，通过两种互补的特征生成器（组合搜索和LLM提议）挖掘可解释的校正特征，并在统一验证门控下筛选特征，显著超越现有特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器在结构化、重复性的方面会失败，而微调修复成本高昂。现有自动化特征工程建模的是数据生成过程，但校正特征应当建模的是模型失败过程，这需要专门的方法来挖掘预测器残差中的可解释特征。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干模型冻结，通过两种互补生成器挖掘残差特征：(1) 对原始输入通道的组合搜索；(2) 由LLM提出命名特征组合、二元标志和短可执行代码。所有候选特征（无论来源）都经过统一的基于验证集的门控机制接受或拒绝，然后由验证集选择的校正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨架上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下都超越了所有专门的特征工程系统，比仅使用校正器的改进约翻倍，并将最弱骨干的误差降低多达27%。这些增益对不同的LLM后端具有鲁棒性，且即使应用在微调后的骨干上也依然保持。

Conclusion: CRAFTER展示了建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程的特征工程范式的有效性，其源无关的流水线还能让先前特征工程系统在完全相同的条件下被评估，成为归因预测改进来源的通用工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [54] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率编程语言，用于构建基于大语言模型（LLM）的应用流程，使开发者能够量化和传播不确定性，并在不修改流程逻辑代码的情况下试验不同的推理扩展技术。论文通过实验研究和Rocq定理证明器的案例研究验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的应用虽然能力强大，但输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量。在包含多次LLM调用和其他工具调用的流程中，这种不确定性会累积放大，使开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。现有方法缺乏对流程级不确定性的系统性量化和传播机制。

Method: 提出一种概率语言（probabilistic language）用于编程LLM-based流程。该语言使开发者能够（1）量化流程中的不确定性，（2）在整个应用流程中传播不确定性，（3）无需修改流程逻辑代码即可实验不同的推理扩展（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该语言的能力，并构建了一个Rocq定理证明器的agent作为案例研究验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率语言为LLM应用提供了一种系统性的不确定性管理框架，提升了复杂多步骤LLM流程的可靠性和可信度，且具有较高的工程灵活性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [55] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一种4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型，通过将感知（确定性代码计算统计量）与描述（LLM将预先计算的事实转化为语言）解耦，解决了多模态时间序列-语言对齐中的自监督陷阱。该模型在多变量理解任务上超越了GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临三重困境：让LLM从时间序列生成描述会导致标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力（自监督陷阱），且大多数数据集只含单变量，无法捕捉多变量间的重要模式（如跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）。这三个问题导致现有方法无法同时兼顾可靠性、真实性和可扩展性。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算统计量，LLM仅负责将这些预计算的事实口头化为语言描述。感知任务（LLM不擅长）交给计算处理，而表达任务由LLM承担。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上取得最佳多变量事实分数（0.283），显著优于GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），且该差距通过Holm校正的配对显著性检验。生成的caption中的可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过计算-语言解耦的范式，CGTime证明可以用较小的模型参数在多变量时间序列理解上超越更大规模的通用模型，为时间序列-语言对齐提供了更可靠、真实且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [56] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与推理显式解耦：使用现成感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据，再通过LoRA微调LLM在这些显式几何证据上进行推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练即可在空间推理基准上取得竞争性性能，同时兼具可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理方法通常通过大规模训练隐式地联合获取3D感知与推理能力，但作者观察到现代感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合与符号推理，二者优势互补，因此提出显式分离感知与推理以替代端到端范式。

Method: DiSR框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用即插即用的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；(2) 采用LoRA技术对LLM进行微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据执行空间推理，无需复杂工具使用策略或大规模3D VQA训练。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上，DiSR无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略即可达到具有竞争力的性能，同时相比端到端方法在可解释性、模块化程度和计算效率方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的新范式，相比端到端建模更具优势，证明了模块化设计在处理空间推理任务中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [57] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中将潜在风格变量边缘分布匹配到高斯先验并不能保证其与类别信息独立。边缘匹配对类条件分布无约束，潜在风格仍可高度预测标签。作者通过精确分解证明消除此不匹配是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，但并非充分条件。实验表明近零全局MMD下线性探针仍可在基准模型中恢复74%-100%的类别标签，并提出多种缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型普遍将风格变量z_s的边缘分布正则化为固定高斯先验，并以此宣称风格表示与类别信息独立。作者认为这一解释是错误的，因为仅匹配边缘分布对类条件分布不施加任何约束，风格变量仍可能携带大量类别信息。该误解可能导致对模型因子化能力的错误评估。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确分解，表明边缘分布匹配仅是因子化采样所需四个条件之一，消除该不匹配是必要的但非充分的。实验上使用一个案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在空间基线，测量全局最大均值差异（MMD）和线性探针分类准确率，并在六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督程度、两个数据集）下验证泄漏的持续性。同时评估四种缓解策略，并提出后验条件先验和经验风格库两种改进方法。

Result: 所有基线模型在近零全局MMD下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率（随机水平10%）恢复类别标签。案例模型聚类准确率达99.15%，但外部评估的类条件生成仅成功16%。四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上实现0.97的类条件生成得分（无需重训练），在CIFAR-10上仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布计算的任何散度都无法认证其与类别标签的独立性。仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中常被声称的性质。研究者需要采用类条件评估或更强的独立性条件来验证模型的因子化能力。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [58] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成方法（角色-任务对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤rubric、结构化验证轨迹）训练多模态大语言模型执行"rubric理解"任务——即模型不是被评估的生成器，而是遵循优先级规则的执行器。仅用10K合成样本即可将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且收益可迁移至多种开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令通常捆绑多项优先级不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题，模型缺乏处理多规则、带优先级指令的能力。作者通过引入"rubric理解"来填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出PRISM，一个四阶段数据合成框架：(1) 生成角色-任务对；(2) 生成前缀引导的规则集（带优先级）；(3) 质量过滤生成高质量rubric；(4) 生成结构化验证轨迹。同时提出PRISM-Eval评估基准，包含Loose和Strict两种指标，通过固定标签的确定性匹配进行评估，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅使用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能。增益可迁移至另外四种开源MLLM（覆盖密集和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化rubric监督是多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，验证了通过合成数据训练模型"遵循而非被评估"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroSFT，一种参数高效的过拟合感知多任务微调框架，将msft的调度器状态从全模型检查点重构为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练主干并只训练注入的适配器，实现了更高的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务有监督微调将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务达到最佳泛化的时间不同。msft通过任务级回滚和排除暴露了这一不匹配，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，使得阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练主干，仅训练注入的适配器；在每个任务达到峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并在剩余的活跃混合任务上继续训练。在层级别，每个适配器采用受AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，将其应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，使得更新在输入上保持线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留主干比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达61.36%，而对应的msft参考行仅为59.85%。AuroSFT在全部五个主干上均取得了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的适配器状态替代全模型检查点，在保持过拟合感知多任务微调性能优势的同时显著降低了状态存储、恢复和部署成本，验证了紧凑可合并适配器状态在多任务SFT中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT方法，在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上附加零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支，使低比特语言模型能够在保持量化兼容性的同时获得输入相关的非线性校正能力，在多项指标上优于QOFT和QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT虽然实现了低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换内，无法进行输入依赖的非线性校正，这限制了模型对复杂任务的表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT保留QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，并在每个适配的线性层上附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差。具体地，它将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基以及有界或token依赖的门控机制。零初始化的up投影使得AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能等价，而正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性存在，而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上比匹配的QOFT提升Macro-6达1.30-2.70%，比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，同时相对于QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。小型考试式选择题数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断使用。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过将非线性残差与量化兼容的正交分支解耦，在保持参数高效性的同时显著提升低比特微调的性能，验证了非线性校正在低比特语言模型适配中的价值。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [61] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowAdam是一种混合优化器，将Adam与基于ODE的连续梯度流集成。当EMA统计量检测到困难地形时，FlowAdam切换到裁剪的ODE积分，并通过"软动量注入"（Soft Momentum Injection）将ODE速度与Adam动量混合以避免训练崩溃。在耦合优化基准上比Adam降低10-22%的泛化误差，在Jester推荐任务上提升6%，同时在CIFAR-10等良态任务上与Adam持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用基于梯度平方指数移动平均的对角坐标预条件器，这种对角缩放依赖坐标系，在面对密集或旋转的参数耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络）时表现不佳，因为它独立处理每个参数，无法捕捉参数间的耦合关系。

Method: FlowAdam由三部分组成：(1) 保留Adam的EMA统计量用于检测地形难度；(2) 当检测到困难地形时切换到裁剪的ODE积分进行连续梯度流集成；(3) 核心创新为Soft Momentum Injection，在模式切换时将ODE速度与Adam动量以加权方式混合，避免朴素混合方法导致的训练崩溃。通过消融实验验证硬替换（hard replacement）会显著降低性能。

Result: 在低秩矩阵/张量恢复任务上减少10-22%的测试误差，在Jester协同过滤数据集上减少6%误差，超越了tuned Lion和AdaBelief；在良态工作负载（CIFAR-10）上与Adam持平。MovieLens-100K证实收益源于耦合参数交互而非偏置估计。消融实验显示软注入至关重要：硬替换将准确率从100%降至82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过在Adam中引入ODE连续梯度流集成，有效解决了自适应矩方法在处理耦合参数时对角线预条件不足的问题，同时通过软动量注入保证了模式切换的稳定性，是一种兼顾耦合优化性能与良态任务鲁棒性的混合优化器。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [62] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的个性化税务感知投资组合管理应用，由FastAPI后端和网页仪表盘组成，通过三段式强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、Mixture-of-Experts分配策略、LoRA适配器）将自然语言投资目标路由到六个投资任务并生成实时经纪商集成推荐。系统已完成端到端集成测试（Alpaca模拟交易），但尚未对真实用户开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理工具。现有机器人顾问采用静态规则分配，而机构级系统要求高账户门槛和复杂技术栈，普通个人投资者无法使用。

Method: 系统采用三段式强化学习架构：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器处理市场数据；(2) Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略配合学习意图路由器，将自然语言目标路由至六个投资任务；(3) 轻量LoRA适配器利用个人经纪行为个性化推荐，无需重训练共享模型。系统包含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的动作完整性链。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过Alpaca模拟交易API端到端集成测试。提供14天滚动前向回测（含bootstrap置信区间）作为部署前初步验证。同时报告了若干工程经验教训：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 这是一个诚实报告为"部署前新兴应用"的系统，具备明确的完整部署路径。研究价值还体现在对构建于外部实时数据源的应用型强化学习系统提供可泛化的工程经验。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [63] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器隐式偏差与度量对称性（gauge symmetry）之间的关系：Adam等坐标式优化器因缺乏规范等变性而无法像梯度下降那样偏向低秩解。作者提出结构定理刻画无记忆等变规则即Gram决定的左预 conditioner，并通过转移定理证明梯度流的路径性质可传递到公共标量流。实验在矩阵感知、Transformer和超光谱数据集上验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在分解模型W=UV^T上隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam从相同小初始化出发却没有这一性质。这一差异的根源此前未被系统解释，理解它对于选择优化器、理解神经网络的隐式正则化至关重要，特别是在矩阵感知和Transformer训练场景下，优化器的选择直接决定了最终解的性质。

Method: 作者指出损失函数在(U,V)->(UQ,VQ)下的规范对称性（gauge symmetry）是关键。梯度流可利用低秩机制当且仅当优化器是规范等变的（gauge-equivariant），这是必要条件而非充分条件。通过结构定理证明记忆less等变规则恰好等价于Gram决定的左预 conditioner；通过转移定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流（common-scalar flows）。随后在欠定矩阵感知上对9种更新规则排序，引入从坐标式到共享标量的单参数族以单调恢复隐式偏差，并用"谱调度"统一了关于Muon的矛盾结论。

Result: 梯度下降、动量、"共享标量"Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足规范等变性；Adam、RMSProp和其他坐标式方法不满足。在Transformer中，Adam在第一步就分开了两个规范等价的初始化，而等变优化器保持在浮点精度内；最终每头不变式W_Q^T W_K的相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在两个超光谱数据集上，在匹配训练损失下，梯度下降在最低采样密度时降低了43-44%的留出误差，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 基的选择（basis choice）不是调参细节，而是一个关于优化器选择哪个插值点（interpolant）的决策。规范等变性是解释梯度下降与Adam在隐式低秩偏向上的根本分歧的关键概念，而各向异性（anisotropy）是破坏这一偏置的具体机制。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）在山火后泥石流预测中的表现，通过SHAP特征重要性分析和TabPFN合成数据增强技术，解决了特征空间重叠、模型可解释性和训练数据有限三大挑战。TabPFN取得最高未增强性能（威胁分数0.637），合成数据增强使除CNN外的所有模型性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山火后泥石流预测对减轻社区和基础设施灾害至关重要，但现有机器学习模型面临三大难题：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠导致分类困难、模型缺乏可解释性、训练数据有限。这些问题限制了预测模型的可靠性和实际应用。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的山火后泥石流观测数据，系统比较15种模型（含TabPFN和基于树的模型），采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能；利用SHAP分析特征重要性；并评估使用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强的效果。

Result: TabPFN在未增强条件下取得最高威胁分数0.637，紧随其后的是最优的基于树的模型；SHAP分析表明短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是预测的关键特征，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较低；合成数据增强使除CNN外的所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁分数提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过结合严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，该工作为山火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，验证了TabPFN在该任务中的优越性，并证明了合成数据增强策略对缓解训练数据稀缺问题的有效性。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [65] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OSPDIM，一种基于Riemannian流形的源自由在线无监督域自适应框架，通过在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数并使用信息最大化进行优化，以解决在线BCI场景中标签分布动态变化（标签偏移）导致的几何错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的BCI系统在跨受试者和跨会话泛化时通常需要无监督域自适应（UDA）。现有的Riemannian对齐方法（如RCT）能有效处理协变量偏移，但隐含假设类别先验均衡。在真实在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术无法正确地在几何上对齐目标数据分布。

Method: OSPDIM将流形约束的偏置参数引入切空间映射，通过信息最大化（information maximization）优化该偏置参数，以实时校正由不平衡数据流引起的几何偏移。与依赖全局批次统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM在线逐帧估计并校正几何偏差。
