Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真验证了OSPDIM能够有效校正标准居中方法无法解决的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM显著优于标准Riemannian基线方法，尤其在类别严重不平衡的挑战性在线适应场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际即插即用的BCI系统提供了鲁棒解决方案，能够在动态标签分布的在线场景中有效处理几何错位问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [66] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）输出头，将预测中的不确定性分解为三种生成器（二值、有界、随机），分别对应离散模式选择、系统性漂移和随机噪声，并通过封闭似然实现跨时间步的代数耦合推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络的概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一共形区域，无法区分真实预测任务中三种不同的不确定性来源：模式间的离散选择、所选模式内有界的系统性漂移，以及不可约的随机噪声。这限制了模型对不确定性结构的理解与利用。

Method: HProbZ将三种不确定性分别表示为zonotope（带状多面体）的二值、有界和随机生成器，并通过卷积获得封闭形式的似然。共享有界生成器可在单次前向传播中将各时间步的预测代数耦合，从而观测一步即可精化其余所有步的预测分布。同时在推理时提供解析的每模式风险和多模态共形集合。

Result: 理论上证明了三种生成器可基于似然在排列意义下被识别，且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合具有表示上的区分性。经验上在代表性预测基准上相对同编码器混合基线表现有效。

Conclusion: HProbZ提供了高斯混合或共形预测器无法同时具备的结构性质（可识别性、表示独特性、代数耦合推理），为多源不确定性建模提供了新的视角与工具。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [67] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，将传统的二值"保留或删除"驱逐策略替换为可恢复的驱逐机制。通过保留高置信度窗口的全精度、存储中间窗口的量化可恢复层、仅删除最低置信度窗口，并结合动态去量化恢复机制，在固定内存预算下显著提升长上下文任务的注意力保留和信息保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型推理受限于KV缓存的内存占用。现有主流的注意力分数驱逐策略隐含一个关键假设：被驱逐的token永远不会再次有用。然而作者发现该假设在解码过程中是脆弱的——随着生成查询的演化，token和窗口的重要性发生漂移，被驱逐的状态可能在后来的解码中重新获得大量注意力权重，造成不可逆的信息丢失。

Method: 1) 提出两个诊断指标：Future Missed Mass（未来被丢弃状态获得的注意力质量）和Global LIR（历史非活跃区域的重新激活程度）；2) 设计QEvict三层KV缓存管理架构：全精度高置信度窗口层、量化中间窗口可恢复层、删除低置信度窗口层；3) 利用累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性，当量化窗口重新变得重要时将其去量化并提升至全精度层。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict相对于代表性的驱逐方法和量化基线持续取得改进，降低了错失注意力的程度，提升了信息保持效果。

Conclusion: 二进制保留删除驱逐策略在解码过程中存在不可逆的信息丢失风险。通过引入可恢复的三层驱逐机制，QEvict在相同内存预算下实现了更全面的历史上下文保持和关键区域的全精度保留，为KV缓存管理提供了兼顾效率与信息完整性新思路。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [68] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA（次梯度驯化未调整Langevin算法），用于从势函数非光滑、梯度超线性增长且非凸的目标分布中采样。该算法直接在次梯度上操作，无需计算昂贵的平滑过程，并通过驯化技术处理超线性区域以获得稳定的显式格式。作者推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数均显式跟踪维度和逆温度，优于现有次梯度Langevin算法的已知速率，并提供了相关优化问题的超额风险估计。此外，该算法在GPT-2系列LLM的预训练势函数上验证了假设，其增强的坐标上升变体在预训练中可与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Langevin采样算法在处理同时满足非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸这三类困难特性的目标分布时缺乏有效方案。传统的平滑处理计算代价高昂，而已有次梯度Langevin算法的收敛速率不够理想。本文旨在设计一种直接基于次梯度的稳定算法，获得更好的非渐近收敛保证，并在实际大规模场景（如LLM预训练）中验证其有效性。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)：采用驯化（taming）技术构造稳定的显式离散格式，直接操作次梯度而无需平滑步骤。推导Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数显式依赖于维度和逆温度；进一步给出优化问题的超额风险估计。通过GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势函数验证假设条件（含显式常数），并开发增强的坐标上升变体进行实证评估。

Result: SG-TULA在Wasserstein-2距离下获得了优于现有次梯度Langevin算法的非渐近收敛速率，所有常数均显式跟踪。在LLM预训练场景中，算法假设被验证成立（含显式常数），其中增强的坐标上升变体SG-TULA在预训练性能上与微调后的AdamW和Muon相当，而后者目前缺乏可比的非渐近收敛保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入SG-TULA，填补了同时处理非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸势函数的Langevin采样算法的理论空白，提供了更优的收敛速率和显式的常数跟踪。更重要的是，该算法不仅在理论上成立，还在LLM预训练这一具有挑战性的实际任务中展现了与现有SOTA优化器相当的性能，体现了理论采样方法在大规模机器学习中的实际潜力。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [69] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种联邦学习模拟器层面的通信节省机制，允许选中的客户端在头部梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献按年龄衰减的缓存更新，同时配合硬缓存年龄限制和最小新鲜客户端配额。在50个虚拟客户端、6个图像数据集、Dirichlet标签异构(α=0.5)、三轮种子的90轮预算下，实现了83.36-85.42%的上行更新数据字段节省（对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），精度差异为-5.29到-0.14个百分点；考虑对称密集模型下行链路后，标题节省降至41.68-42.71%。作者明确声明该机制系模拟器层面的特征刻画，不声称无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少或性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在每一轮迭代中反复经历本地优化和模型更新传输，通信开销巨大。现有方法（如Top-K稀疏化）已能在一定程度上减少上行通信，但复用缓存更新以进一步节省通信的潜力尚未得到充分探索。作者希望设计一种机制，在保证客户端参与度和新鲜度的前提下，通过复用年龄衰减的缓存更新来降低上行通信开销，并明确限定研究范围，避免过度声称。

Method: DG-FedReuse的核心机制包括：(1) 基于随机头部梯度差异代理（stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy）与轮次相关阈值比较，决定是否允许选中客户端贡献缓存更新；(2) 设定硬缓存年龄上限（hard cache-age limit）和最小新鲜客户端配额（minimum fresh-client quota）作为约束条件；(3) 新鲜更新采用自适应逐张量Top-K数值字段表示（adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation）。实验设置包括6个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异构(α=0.5)和三颗种子；同时进行对称密集模型下行链路敏感性分析。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现了83.36-85.42%的建模更新数据字段上行节省，优于匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；种子对齐精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。测试控制检查点下的最佳测试精度仅作为探索性档案证据保留，相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。融入对称密集模型下行链路后，标题节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点，表明通信结论高度依赖会计边界的选择。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面证明了缓存更新复用结合Top-K稀疏表示的通信节省潜力，但作者明确界定研究边界：该工作仅刻画了所提复用规则在实现模拟器中的行为，并未建立无偏泛化性、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或对现有过期更新和惰性聚合方法的优势。通信效率指标对上行/下行记账边界的敏感性提醒研究者和实践者在评估联邦学习通信优化时需谨慎界定测量范围。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [70] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种势流框架PMOT（Potential Matching Optimal Transport），用于一般p-代价最优传输问题（c_p(x,y)=‖x-y‖^p）。PMOT用标量势参数化连续归一化流的速度场，通过自诱导匹配损失沿直线桥训练势梯度，实现灵活终端分布匹配。理论证明零损失解满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统并恢复p-最优传输映射。实验验证了p-特定映射的正确性及在高维表格数据和颜色变换上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优传输方法大多聚焦于2-Wasserstein距离（p=2），缺乏对一般p-代价最优传输的统一框架。作者希望构建一个能够处理任意p值的最优传输框架，同时保证理论上的最优性严格性，并在实践中可扩展、灵活。

Method: PMOT将CNF（连续归一化流）速度场参数化为广义Benamou-Brenier形式中的标量势梯度。通过自诱导匹配损失（self-induced matching loss），模型沿自身端点确定的直线桥训练势梯度，同时允许灵活的终端分布匹配。理论部分建立了零损失精确性定理：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统。

Result: 在合成基准上，PMOT学习的p-特定映射与对应的p-匹配OT参考一致。在高维表格数据上作为似然密度模型具有竞争力，MMD颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT为一般p-代价最优传输提供了统一、理论严格且实践可行的框架，兼具最优传输的理论保证和现代深度学习模型的灵活性，在密度建模与分布匹配任务上均表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [71] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一个基于注意力机制的框架，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该方法将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，通过成对患者比较并结合区间值概率来处理右删失数据，在非线性合成数据集上优于元学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析场景中，个体化治疗获益估计对精准医疗至关重要，但现有元学习基线（如T-learner和S-learner）在非线性特征空间中表现显著退化，且难以恰当地处理右删失观测数据中的不确定性。

Method: Surv-IPTB将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较。框架通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原理性地处理右删失观测，并引入具有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现灵活的成对比较聚合，同时为删失病例学习软类别概率。

Result: 在具有螺旋、钟形和圆形等复杂非线性结构的合成数据集上，该模型在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持稳健性能，一致优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的元学习基线（T-learner和S-learner），特别是在传统方法显著退化的非线性场景中。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架被证明是一种可扩展且统计上原理性的解决方案，适用于生存环境中的个性化治疗获益评估，且代码已公开。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [72] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），将量子理论中的数学结构（复值表示、密度矩阵式隐状态）引入世界建模，并验证了其作为归纳偏置的有效性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐状态或Transformer激活表示隐状态，作者探索源自量子理论的数学结构能否为世界建模提供有用的归纳偏置。

Method: 引入QSWM框架，包含结构化隐状态、潜在转移算子及测量启发的解码映射；建立经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧凑性三个基础性质；实现复值变体和密度矩阵式变体两种QSWM。

Result: 在初等细胞自动机上与强经典基线对比，复值QSWMs展现出有前景的局部预测潜力，但长时程展开和密度矩阵式变体存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 量子启发的复值表示在世界建模的局部预测上具有优势，但长时程预测和密度矩阵式表示仍需进一步改进，说明量子数学结构作为归纳偏置有潜力但尚不成熟。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [73] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过凸学习方法从非线性动力系统观测中提取紧凑线性状态空间表示（LDS）的端到端可证明流水线：先用观测谱滤波（OSF）学习隐式谱预测器，再通过谱到LDS蒸馏将其转化为显式循环线性动力系统。理论保证表明预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习项，且与潜在维度无关。实验在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆中验证了该流水线的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接求解非凸的系统辨识问题在理论和方法上都极具挑战性。作者希望回答一个关键问题：能否通过紧致的线性状态空间表示来学习非线性动力系统，而无需直接求解非凸优化问题？这一问题的解决将为非线性系统学习提供理论上有保障的替代路径。

Method: 方法包含两大步骤：(1) 观测谱滤波（OSF）：采用凸优化方法学习一个隐式谱预测器，其目标是与系统的最佳线性观测器竞争；(2) 谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）：将学到的隐式预测器转化为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论分析表明，蒸馏后的LDS的平均预测误差可分解为指数衰减的蒸馏误差项和由最佳观测器的Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习误差项，且该保证与维度无关。

Result: 理论层面，论文给出了首个端到端可证明的方法，即通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏来提取非线性动力学的最佳事后LDS表示，其误差界依赖于观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。实验层面，在标准线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上，train-then-distill流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器在性能上匹配或超越了直接训练（非凸方法）的基线模型。

Conclusion: 本文证明了非线性动力系统可以通过"凸学习+可证明蒸馏"的流水线被紧凑的线性状态空间模型有效表示，从而绕开了非凸系统辨识的困难。该框架的误差保证是维度无关的，且实验验证了其在多个任务上的实用性和竞争力，为非线性系统辨识提供了新的理论基石和方法范式。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [74] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FALM-PINN优化框架，通过交替训练解耦表示学习与系数拟合，利用傅里叶增强基函数和Levenberg-Marquardt算法解决PINN在高频、多尺度及强非线性PDE中的谱偏差和表示系数耦合问题，在多个挑战性数值实验中相对L2误差较SOTA低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在求解高频、多尺度解及强非线性PDE时常常失效，根本原因有两个：一是谱偏差导致神经网络欠拟合高频特征；二是表示学习与系数拟合在单一非凸优化目标中相互耦合纠缠。现有方法未能有效解耦这两个因素。

Method: 提出FALM-PINN双层优化框架：上层问题学习傅里叶增强基，将高频分量注入潜在空间以丰富表示能力；下层问题利用Levenberg-Marquardt算法在所得基上拟合投影系数，将系数拟合转化为非线性最小二乘问题。该框架适用于一般非线性及耦合PDE系统，对线性PDE可退化为单步凸优化问题，并证明了交替训练方案在两种情况下的全局收敛性。

Result: 在多个高频和非线性PDE数值实验中，FALM-PINN的相对L2误差比最先进的基线方法低至两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过解耦表示学习与系数拟合，FALM-PINN有效缓解了PINN的谱偏差和表示-系数耦合问题，在保持全局收敛性保证的同时显著提升了高频和非线性PDE的求解精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [75] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需分组标注即可在模型收敛后识别虚假相关样本的方法。核心思想是：模型对虚假相关一致的样本采用共享规则进行分类，而对其余样本则通过输入特异的配置拟合，后者对固定扰动更脆弱。通过对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，多次前向传播即可区分两类样本，无需早期训练信号或早停轮次。在Waterbirds上将最差分组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实分组标签的85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在含虚假相关数据上虽然平均准确率高，但在相关不成立的子群体上表现差。现有识别受影响样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需要选择干预轮次这一超参数，且通常需要分组标注的验证集来调参，这限制了实用性。

Method: 提出一种基于扰动敏感性的后收敛样本识别方法。关键观察：收敛后损失无法区分两类群体；与虚假相关一致的样本由共享决策规则分类，而其余样本由输入特异的脆弱配置拟合。对收敛模型输入施加固定扰动，后者预测翻转的频率远高于前者。该方法仅需每个训练样本两次前向传播，不依赖分组标注，也无需早停轮次。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，利用检测出的样本进行再平衡训练，最差分组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实分组标签可达85.8%，性能接近上界。

Conclusion: 模型收敛后的扰动敏感性可作为识别虚假相关样本的有效信号，避免了早期训练信号和分组标注的依赖，为在无组标注场景下提升最差分组性能提供了一种简单高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [76] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [77] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架（AFlowNets）从不完全信息博弈扩展到不完全信息博弈。作者证明原始AFlowNets的约束在不完全信息博弈下无法获得有效的策略密度和训练目标，并提出了严格泛化AFlowNets的IFlowNets方法。初步实验表明，IFlowNets在三个标准博弈环境中性能与速度上与OSMCCFR和标准RL方法相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的博弈论算法大多将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以平衡计算速度和性能，但针对不完全信息博弈中的生成式采样框架研究较少。作者希望探索生成式流网络在不完全信息博弈中的适用性。

Method: 作者将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）扩展至不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络（IFNs）。首先通过理论分析证明原始AFlowNets建立的约束在不完全信息博弈中无法得到有效的策略密度和训练目标，随后提出IFlowNets作为其严格泛化版本，以解决这一问题。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验结果中，IFlowNets在性能和速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）和标准RL方法相比，表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功地将生成流网络框架推广到不完全信息博弈，理论层面证明了AFlowNets的局限性并给出了泛化方案，实证层面验证了其与现有方法的竞争力，为该领域提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [78] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示生成式训练中"探索建模"（XM）的第三轴实为"自由度"（行为约束的弱度），而非生成表达性。作者证明XM损失与候选遗漏可接受区域的概率相关，探索将遗漏概率提升至K次幂；实验表明XM确实在优化自由度，且基于自由度的选择器在30个案例中29个胜过验证选择，在分布偏移下显著改进XM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式训练中，每次比较只产生一个输出，导致产生常见答案的模型可能胜过保留更广泛行为谱的模型。作者引入Explorative Modeling（XM）通过每次产生K个输出并在最接近的候选上更新来克服此问题，声称探索是"第三条预训练轴"。然而，作者认为需要澄清这条轴的本质，将其从"生成表达性"重新概念化为"自由度"，以揭示其泛化意义的真正来源。

Method: 1) 理论证明：推导XM平均损失与候选遗漏可接受区域概率的关系，证明探索将遗漏概率提升至K次幂；2) 数学分析：证明对K>1，匹配概率随自由度上升；3) 经验验证：设计Forward XM实验测试不同K值下的自由度变化；4) 对比实验：训练XM候选池，将基于验证集的选择与仅读取未标注父上下文的自由度选择器进行30次案例对比。

Result: 理论结果表明XM损失取决于候选遗漏可接受区域的概率，探索使其按K次幂衰减。实证结果：较大K值增加或饱和了测量的自由度，且在上下文相关目标下每个测试值都增加自由度；自由度选择器在30个案例中29个胜过验证选择；在分布偏移下选择自由度显著改善XM表现。

Conclusion: XM（探索）是实现自由度的手段，自由度才是最终目标。生成表达性只是自由度的模式计数代理，其放弃了赋予自由度泛化意义的扩展结构。选择自由度而非MDL能显著提升归纳实验中的泛化性能（110-500%），并在分布偏移下改进XM。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [79] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL框架，通过离线学习"harness策略"来解决长时程LLM智能体的外部执行支持问题。该框架将信念（Belief）、进度（Progress）和经验（Experience）作为策略可见的外部状态，结合监督微调与成本感知GRPO训练，在ALFWorld上达到96.9%的成功率，并揭示了"harness退火"（训练将重复模式内化为模型策略）和"harness演化"（状态被精炼为紧凑的自适应基板）两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体依赖外部执行支持来维护状态、追踪进度、调用工具、验证结果和复用经验。然而，有效使用外部工作空间面临两个耦合挑战：从噪声交互轨迹中形成状态，以及对外部状态访问的运行时控制。现有方法依赖提示、启发式或领域特定约定，导致外部工作空间及其使用策略被手工设计，缺乏系统性学习方法。

Method: 提出harness策略学习问题，即智能体离线学习harness策略，在线任务执行时部署以构建和更新外部状态。具体方法包括：(1) 将Belief、Progress、Experience（BPE）暴露为策略面对的外部harness状态；(2) 通过监督harness微调教授基础智能体harness动作空间及如何构建有用的外部状态；(3) 使用成本感知的GRPO探索协调策略，在长时程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。在ALFWorld上基于Qwen3-8B实现。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到96.9%的成功率。揭示两个关键动态：harness退火——训练将反复出现的harness使用模式内化为模型策略，使智能体从频繁调用harness转向选择性外部状态访问；harness演化——进度更新和经验整合将harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基板。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体能从可训练的外部harness工作空间构建与协调策略中获益，这种能力超越了单纯添加更强工具或更大记忆的范式，表明外部状态管理本身可以也应当被学习而非手工设计。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [80] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力机制中"上下文刚性"（输入相关的softmax路由与输入无关的线性值投影之间的不对称性）对排列不变集合目标的影响，提出了Transformation Degrees of Freedom（TDOF）复杂度度量，证明了上下文刚性注意力需要与TDOF成比例的深度，而上下文自适应值族可在单层内表示相同目标。基于此提出Matrix Zonotopic Attention（MZAttn）——用上下文自适应的矩阵-zonotope族替换固定值投影，在初始化时退化为标准多头注意力，保持排列等变性。实验表明其优势具有选择性：在高秩、稀疏组合性目标上显著，在聚合统计目标上与标准注意力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力将输入相关的softmax路由与输入无关的线性值投影相结合，导致每个样本的操作符映射（将聚合值映射到输出）对所有输入集都是相同的。这种不对称性对排列不变集合目标的表达效率产生何种影响，此前缺乏系统的理论分析。作者希望从复杂度理论层面理解这种"上下文刚性"的局限，并设计更灵活但保持等变性的注意力变体。

Method: 1) 提出TDOF（Transformation Degrees of Freedom）作为目标操作符的复杂度度量，统计精确表示所需的输入相关方向数。2) 给出深度分离分析：上下文刚性注意力需要深度与TDOF成正比；而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示相同目标。3) 提出MZAttn：用中心矩阵加由输入相关门控加权的生成矩阵之和（矩阵-zonotope族）替换固定值投影，初始化时退化为标准多头注意力，保持排列等变性，并具有数据驱动的可达性解释。

Result: 在多种集合预测任务上的实验与TDOF预测一致：架构优势具有选择性——对高秩、稀疏组合性依赖输入集的目标，MZAttn优势明显；而在聚合统计类目标上，参数匹配的标准自注意力已具竞争力，优势较小。

Conclusion: 标准多头注意力的"上下文刚性"是其在某些排列不变集合目标上表达效率受限的根源。TDOF提供了量化这一局限的理论框架，而上下文自适应的值族（如MZAttn）能更高效地表示高TDOF目标。然而这种优势并非普遍适用，而是取决于目标对输入集的依赖方式——在高秩、稀疏组合场景下才能体现架构改进的价值。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [81] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-Skill，一种将任务知识外部化分解为可独立加载/卸载/共享的算子（通过轻量接口被冻结语言模型读取）的设计空间，支持从文本技能转换（注册）或从任务结果直接学习（奖励学习）两条路径，在十个基准、四个骨干网络上显著优于文本提示、SoftPrefix、Prefix Tuning和LoRA等基线，且支持多技能无遗忘共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务知识通常以文本存储在提示中（模块化但每次使用需重新解析）或以权重更新存储在模型中（能力难以独立加载、移除或共享）。这两种范式存在根本的模块性与可移植性矛盾，需要一个外部因子化的知识存储与读取机制。

Method: KV-Skill设计了一个外部因子化算子的设计空间，冻结的骨干语言模型通过轻量接口读取该算子。包含两条互补路径：(1) 注册：将作者提供的文本技能转换为文本派生的算子，并训练共享的每骨干接口；(2) 奖励学习：直接从任务结果开发紧凑的潜在算子（可选结合作者技能）。两条路径均不增加提示中的位置。此外通过秩分析验证单任务对齐方向即可保留大部分收益，并以单一共享接口承载多个独立可加载技能。

Result: 在10个基准和3个模型家族的4个骨干网络上，文本转KV-Skill一致地使程序性知识更有效。在Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上，注册达到77.2准确率，远超源文本技能23.4、SkillOpt 52.0和SoftSkill 64.5。在匹配的奖励训练与参数预算下，KV-Skill在8个匹配设置中的7个优于soft prefix、prefix tuning和LoRA。秩分析表明文本派生算子几乎完全保留收益（每注入层一个任务对齐方向），而随机方向失效。单一共享接口可保留三个独立可加载KV-Skill且无可测遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可以从文本或经验中获取、压缩为外部算子，并与骨干网络分离部署。KV-Skill在保持骨干冻结的同时实现了比文本提示和权重微调更高的有效性与更好的模块化/可移植性，展示了外在化知识存储作为任务能力承载的新范式的可行性。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [82] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG 是一种零训练（training-free）的检索增强预测方法，通过对检索到的过去-未来窗口对施加闭式幅值重缩放（amplitude rescaling）和整数相位平移（integer-lag phase shift），使冻结的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）无需可学习的融合模块即可动态利用检索上下文。在七个标准基准数据集上，Align-RAG 以无参方式超越了当前最优的基于训练的自适应器（平均 MSE 降低 3.75%），并在四种冻结 TSFM 上将零样本 MSE 提升 2.5%-13.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强时间序列预测方法通常依赖训练好的融合模块（如自适应器）来合并检索示例，其隐含假设是冻结的 TSFM 主干无法自主动态整合检索上下文。本文旨在检验并推翻这一假设，证明无需任何可学习参数，冻结主干本身即具备在上下文中利用检索示例的能力。

Method: Align-RAG 不训练任何参数，仅对每个检索到的（过去，未来）窗口对施加闭式（closed-form）的逐对幅值重缩放和整数延迟相位平移，然后将其注入冻结 TSFM 的上下文。同时通过对比冻结模型在对齐演示下的预测偏移与对应闭式岭回归（ridge）的预测偏移，并结合未来打乱（future-shuffle）控制实验，从机制上分析对齐为何有效。

Result: 在冻结 Chronos-Bolt 上，Align-RAG 在七个标准基准数据集上平均 MSE 较最优的已训练检索自适应器降低 3.75%；在另外四种不同架构的冻结 TSFM 上，零样本 MSE 提升 2.5% 至 13.7%（无需针对每个主干调参）。机制分析显示，对齐示例诱导的预测偏移与同对上的闭式岭回归预测器预测偏移高度吻合，且未来打乱对照排除了"未来平均"解释。

Conclusion: 冻结的 TSFM 已经内在地支持对检索内容的动态上下文利用，先前归因于学习融合模块的性能提升可以完全通过零训练的方式恢复。闭式对齐应成为训练任何融合模块之前检索增强预测的默认基线。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [83] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [84] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE是一个轻量级反事实门控框架，用于不完整多模态分类。它通过泰勒证据分数在单次前向-后向传播中估计每个细粒度证据单元的替换反事实效应，并转化为连续门控和注意力logit偏置，在不修改主干架构的前提下实现单元级证据调制。在六个基准上优于强基线，并通过泰勒余项理论分析保证了方法的原理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态不完整处理（插补与动态融合）在粗粒度的模态层面操作，无法在同一恢复模态内保留可靠组件同时抑制误导性组件，从而影响预测可靠性。这促使研究者在细粒度（单元级）层面进行证据控制。

Method: GAUGE包含三个核心步骤：(1) 使用冻结的插补器填充缺失模态，并将观测与恢复的输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元；(2) 通过预测感知的泰勒证据分数，在单次前向-后向传播中近似计算每个单元被参考表示替换时的反事实效应，无需显式干预每个单元；(3) 将分数映射为连续门控，并转化为加性注意力logit偏置，实现单元级证据调制而不改变主干架构。此外还提供了泰勒余项理论分析来表征一阶近似的误差界。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GAUGE在多种不完整输入设置下均优于强基线方法。理论分析表明一阶泰勒近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差可通过泰勒余项刻画，验证了方法的原理性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: GAUGE为不完整多模态分类提供了一种轻量、可扩展且有理论保障的细粒度证据控制方案。通过泰勒展开的单次传播近似替代了显式单元级干预，既降低了计算开销又实现了细粒度可靠性调节，为多模态不完整场景下的证据调控建立了原则性框架。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [85] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三流检测器（three-stream detector），结合了轨迹位移（motion）与两种受限状态视图（向量量化的粗粒度区域读取器和归一化多层状态的细粒度方向读取器），用于区分语言模型的推理正确与推理错误，在未见过的推理基准上相比仅用位移的最新方法提升了最多12%的选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益用于需要可验证推理的任务，可靠地区分合理推理与有缺陷推理成为重要的实际问题。现有的基于轨迹位移（trajectory-based）方法虽然通过层间残差位移捕捉表示变化并衰减稳定的token特定信息，但位移忽略了更新所源自的状态（location）。然而，恢复完整状态又可能重新引入易受捷径（shortcut）影响的信息。作者识别出这一权衡，并旨在设计一种既能利用状态上下文又不过度回归完整状态探测的方法。

Method: 提出三流检测器（three-stream detector），包含三个互补的信息流：(1) 运动流（motion），基于层间残差位移；(2) 粗粒度区域读取器（coarse region reader），基于向量量化（vector quantization）对状态进行区域化编码；(3) 细粒度方向读取器（fine direction reader），基于归一化多层状态读取方向信息。三流结合，在不过度暴露完整状态的前提下恢复足够的状态上下文以解释运动信号。

Result: 在训练时未见过的推理基准上，该方法的选择准确率相比仅用位移的最新方法（state of the art）提升了最多12%，相比单层探测基线提升21%。尽管仅在推理基准上训练，该方法在事实补全和事实验证任务上也优于所有对比的检测器，表明捕获的信号与正确性（correctness）相关而非特定于某种推理类型。消融实验进一步表明运动、区域和方向三者提供互补信号。

Conclusion: 推理有效性更适合从状态条件化的运动（state-conditioned motion）中读取，而非仅从静态状态或去上下文化的轨迹中独立读取。该工作揭示了位置信息（location）在推理正确性检测中的价值，并提供了一个兼顾状态上下文与快捷信息规避的实用架构。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [86] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建RENDEQ渲染等价集生成器，以科学图为载体直接测量模型对扰动输入的一致性与正确性之间的耦合关系，发现一致性在不同场景下单向有效，且在自训练中反而损害精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将模型对扰动输入的一致性既作为无标签可靠性信号又作为自训练目标，但二者的耦合关系极少被直接测量。自然图像扰动无法保证语义等价，且缺乏精确答案键来定位错误。科学图因其由程序从数据绘制、重绘天然语义等价且有精确答案，为本问题提供了理想实验载体。

Method: 构建RENDEQ渲染等价集生成器，生成语义等价但渲染方式不同（如绘图库等风格因子）的图像集。在三个开源VLM上测量一致性-正确性耦合，每个结论在三个独立实例中复现。实验比较重渲染与重采样的精度和可靠性，将一致性对照均值token对数概率基线，并开展基于跨渲染共识的自监督微调实验。

Result: 重渲染优于重采样；一致性在三个模型中的两个优于证据携带基线（均值token对数概率），第三个持平。方差集中于绘图库这一风格因子，其影响是次大因子的两倍多、比噪声底高一个数量级。基于自身跨渲染共识的微调在所有五个复现中精度均下降，与自然图像上的公开结果符号相反。

Conclusion: 一致性仅在模型错误扩散程度低于某一阈值时才能认证正确性；以一致性为奖励的目标恰好破坏了这种扩散特性，导致一致性认证失效。图一致性-正确性耦合的符号与自然图像场景相反，说明该耦合并非普适不变，依赖数据分布与错误结构。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [87] [Consistency Has a Computable Blind Spot: A Commutation Theory of Label-Free Reliability for Vision-Language Figure Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05675)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于等变关系的无标签可靠性检测方法（Equivariance-Consistency Score），理论证明不变性方法存在系统性盲区——可计算的联合中心化子表征了无法被扰动探测到的错误；配对等变编辑可完备检测仿射读取错误，循环重新标注可弥补标签置换的大部分缺口，并发布REND-EQUIV数据集验证理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不变性的无标签视觉-语言模型可靠性检测存在系统盲区：某些错误在输入扰动下仍保持一致读数，从而被错误地"认证"为正确。作者证明这种盲区是可计算表征的（而非仅存在性结论），并指出需要转向互补的等变关系来捕捉这类错误。

Method: 理论层面：定义"联合中心化子"（joint centralizer）刻画扰动无法探测的错误集合；构建等变关系（equivariance），即编辑数据后正确答案的已知变化量。证明两个匹配编辑对仿射读取错误完备，交换编辑对标签置换不完备，而循环重标注可弥补大部分缺口。实践层面：实现Equivariance-Consistency Score作为无标签、免训练检测器，发布REND-EQUIV数据集。

Result: 理论预测的排序在三个模型上成立；手工标注的测试人群排除了选择循环性问题；第二种不变性方法验证了盲区属于关系本身而非实现细节；循环重标注在实际样本上兑现了预测增益。此外，该表征还解释了分类器变形测试文献中报告的顺序反转现象。

Conclusion: 可检测性是不变性/等变关系与故障类别的联合属性，而非关系单独决定。等变一致性评分为无标签可靠性检测提供了比纯不变性方法更强的理论保证和可计算性。

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [88] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEAM（Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines）是一个生成器无关的框架，用于验证科学机器学习中局部解释能否组装为全局一致的、可被接受的科学解释。通过有限解释层（explanation-sheaf）实例化，SEAM将区域结构化解释的局部一致性计算转化为可计算问题，并在19个实验（包括合成偏微分方程系统和分布外FNO监控）中成功检测出不兼容解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习通常在子域、基准切分或单次预测层面验证模型，但这类局部检查无法确定局部解释能否组装成一个全局可接受的解释。这种局部到全局的一致性问题是科学解释可靠性验证中的关键缺口。

Method: SEAM采用生成器无关框架，通过有限解释-sheaf实例化（SEAM-Ω）将各区域表示为包含状态、闭包、观测通道及可选合约元数据的结构化解释。框架比较相邻解释的重叠部分，将不一致转换为通道解析的阻碍（obstruction），通过限制修复到特定账户允许的修订来测试竞争性解释。该框架还区分不一致性与不可识别性，并监控学习生成器在分布偏移下的表现。

Result: 论文建立了最小代价干预和保护合约可检测性的定理，以及可识别性和闭包可恢复性的辅助结果。在涉及合成偏微分方程系统和分布外傅里叶神经算子（FNO）监控的19个实验中，SEAM能够检测出不兼容解释（即使局部预测准确），并将失败归因到特定通道和重叠区域。

Conclusion: SEAM为现有求解器和学习模型增加了全局解释一致性审计能力，检验其局部解释能否构成连贯的科学解释，为科学机器学习中的解释验证提供了从局部到全局的完整解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [89] [Spectral Aliasing Pretext: A novel task for Self-Supervised fault diagnosis in rotating machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05705)
*Victor Gialis,Maxime Metz,David Esteve,Abdenour Soualhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于频谱混叠的自监督学习方法SAP，通过在未标注振动数据上对Transformer进行预训练，使模型学习频率域不变特征，在线性探针设置下仅用少量标注数据即可获得优于全监督训练的诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在机械故障诊断中表现出色，但依赖大量标注数据，而工业场景中标注数据稀缺，迫切需要一种能够利用未标注数据来提升诊断性能的方法。

Method: SAP方法通过故意对振动信号进行欠采样以产生频谱折叠（混叠），然后训练Transformer模型从折叠后的频谱重建原始未折叠频谱。这个预训练任务迫使模型学习与机械故障相关的频率域不变特征，避免了可能破坏信号的增强方法。

Result: 在CWRU数据集上的实验表明，SAP学习到的表征具有稳定且高度可判别性。在线性探针设置下，仅用一小部分标注数据即可快速达到很高的分类性能且方差较低。相比之下，全量微调（包括全监督训练）并未带来更稳定或更好的结果。

Conclusion: SAP结合线性探针在标注数据有限的情况下，比全监督训练更有效、更可靠，为机械故障诊断提供了一种可行的自监督预训练范式。

Abstract: Deep learning is a new way for machinery fault diagnosis but requires extensive labeled data, a scarce resource in industrial settings. We propose Spectral Aliasing Pretext (SAP), a self-supervised learning method that pretrains models on unlabeled vibration data by exploiting spectral aliasing. We deliberately undersample signals to create folded spectrum, then train a Transformer to reconstruct the original unfolded spectrum. This pretext task forces the model to learn frequency-domain invariants characteristic of mechanical faults, without potentially destructive augmentations. Experiments on the CWRU dataset show that SAP learns stable and highly discriminative representations. In a linear probing setting, SAP quickly achieves very high classification performance with only a small fraction of labeled data and low variance. In contrast, full fine-tuning, including fully supervised training, does not lead to more stable or better results. Overall, these findings suggest that SAP combined with linear probing can be more effective and reliable than fully supervised training for fault diagnosis with limited labeled data.

</details>


### [90] [CircuitSteer: Geometrically Aligned Multi-Layer Steering via Sparse Autoencoder Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05732)
*Mehrshad Saadatinia,Parsa Razmara,Ardalan Aryashad,Ali Abbasi,Seyedarmin Azizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitSteer 是一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAEs）的多层电路干预框架，通过识别跨层分布的语义电路并合成稠密转向向量进行多点干预，实现对LLM行为的更稳健控制。在毒性、情绪强度、谄媚和拒绝等多个任务上的实验表明，它是唯一能持续保持文本质量的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转向方法（如CAA）依赖聚合激活差异的固定单层干预，无法在语义多样的输入上保持一致的行为改变，且跨层效果难以维持，导致转向效果受限。

Method: （1）利用SAEs识别跨多层分布的连贯语义电路；（2）基于特征共激活和解码器方向的几何对齐构建特征流电路，隔离目标行为对应的多层子电路；（3）从稀疏特征合成稠密转向向量；（4）应用多点干预引导模型内部语义轨迹。

Result: 在毒性、情绪强度、谄媚和拒绝任务及两个模型家族上，CircuitSteer是唯一能一致保持流畅文本质量的方法；竞争方法要么牺牲文本质量要么缺乏覆盖度，在谄媚和拒绝等复杂行为上完全失效。

Conclusion: 通过强制所选特征间的几何对齐实现的多层电路转向，比静态单点干预产生更鲁棒和有效的行为控制。

Abstract: Controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for AI alignment. Existing steering methods, such as Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), typically rely on fixed single-layer interventions derived from aggregate activation differences. These methods impose a single intervention across semantically diverse inputs and often fail to sustain consistent behavioral changes across layers, limiting the effectiveness of the steering. In this work, we introduce CircuitSteer, a novel framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and manipulate coherent semantic circuits distributed across multiple layers. By constructing a feature flow circuit based on feature co-activation and the geometric alignment of decoder directions, we isolate the specific multi-layer subcircuits responsible for a target behavior. We then synthesize dense steering vectors from these sparse features and apply multi-point interventions to guide the model's internal semantic trajectory. We evaluate CircuitSteer using contrastive examples across a diverse set of tasks, including toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, and refusal, spanning two model families. Across all models and datasets, CircuitSteer is the only method to consistently produce fluency-preserving interventions; competing methods either sacrifice text quality or lack coverage, failing entirely on complex behaviors like sycophancy and refusal. These results demonstrate that multi-layer circuit steering, enabled by enforcing geometric alignment among selected features, yields strictly more robust and effective behavioral control than static single-point interventions. Code is available at https://github.com/mehrshad-sdtn/CircuitSteer.

</details>


### [91] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting needs Cross Variable Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05742)
*Kuiye Ding,Yifan Hu,Hanchen Wang,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CvLoss（跨变量损失），一种即插即用的结构正则化器，通过对预报残差在跨变量图上施加一致性约束，弥补多变量时间序列预测中直接预测（DF）目标与跨变量及滞后依赖之间的目标差，提升多种预测模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测中，未来变量在共享系统动态下共同演化，但现有方法主要关注历史观测中跨变量的依赖关系，忽略未来值之间的依赖。现代预测模型普遍采用直接预测（DF）范式，使用逐点目标生成多步预测，未显式约束跨变量结构，导致DF目标在存在跨变量和滞后依赖时存在目标差（objective gap）。

Method: 作者提出CvLoss（跨变量损失），一种即插即用的结构正则化器，在跨变量图上约束预测残差。CvLoss通过惩罚预测patch上不一致的边级残差差异，同时在同步和异步交互中鼓励一致性，从而弥补DF目标的不足。

Result: 实验表明，CvLoss能够持续改进具有竞争力的预测模型，优于代表性的学习目标方法，并且与多种预测骨干（backbone）架构兼容。

Conclusion: 通过在预测目标中引入跨变量结构正则化，CvLoss有效弥补了直接预测范式在跨变量和滞后依赖下的目标差，为多变量时间序列预测提供了一种通用且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting presents unique challenges because future variables often co-evolve under shared system dynamics. While existing studies mainly focus on cross-variable dependencies in historical observations, dependencies among future values are much less explored. Specifically, modern forecasting models largely follow the Direct Forecasting (DF) paradigm, generating multi-step forecasts with point-wise objectives that do not explicitly constrain cross-variable structure. In this work, we show that the DF objective is mismatched in the presence of cross-variable and lagged dependencies, revealing an objective gap. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{C}ross-\textbf{V}ariable \textbf{Loss} (CvLoss), a plug-in structural regularizer that constrains forecast residuals on a cross-variable graph. CvLoss penalizes inconsistent edge-wise residual differences over forecast patches, encouraging consistency across both synchronous and asynchronous interactions. Our experiments show that CvLoss consistently improves competitive forecasting models, outperforms representative learning objectives, and is compatible with a variety of forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [92] [Equipment-centric workpiece localization in near real-time using deep learning-based vision and event-driven finite state machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05744)
*Dohyeon Kong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向热锻车间的设备中心框架，通过多静态2D摄像机感知搬运设备，结合关键点引导注意力机制和事件驱动有限状态机，在无需直接追踪工件的情况下实现工件连续定位（平均定位误差317.8mm，事件检测准确率100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热锻过程中工件的连续定位对可追溯性和工序协调至关重要，但极端温度、表面退化及不规则路径使得直接追踪不可靠，亟需一种间接的、鲁棒的工件定位方法。

Method: 采用设备中心（equipment-centric）思路，通过多静态2D相机观测搬运设备，估计其在楼层平面空间中的3D坐标，并识别抓取与释放动作；结合关键点引导的注意力机制（集成于3D CNN）提升动作识别精度，由事件驱动有限状态机将动作校验为离散搬运事件，持续更新工件状态与位置。

Result: 在实际运行的热锻工厂中，事件检测在33秒容忍窗口内达到100%准确率，平均定位误差为317.8mm，系统平均延迟为21秒。

Conclusion: 该框架成功连接了基于视觉的感知与可解释的事件驱动推理，支持工件转移的可视化以及设备操作的定量分析，为热锻场景提供了替代直接追踪的可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous workpiece localization is essential for traceability and process coordination in hot forging, but direct tracking is unreliable because of extreme temperatures, surface degradation, and irregular routing. This study presents an equipment-centric framework that infers workpiece locations from handling equipment observed by multiple static 2D cameras. The framework estimates floorplan-space 3D equipment coordinates and recognizes grasp and release activities. Event-driven finite state machines validate these activities as discrete handling events and continuously update workpiece states and locations. A keypoint-guided attention mechanism integrated into a 3D convolutional neural network improves activity recognition by focusing on functionally relevant equipment regions. Evaluation in an operational hot forging factory achieved 100\% event detection accuracy within a 33-second tolerance window, a mean localization error of 317.8 mm, and a mean system latency of 21 seconds. The framework connects vision-based perception with interpretable event-driven reasoning and supports visualization of workpiece transfers and quantitative analysis of equipment operations.

</details>


### [93] [Accelerating nanodrug development in continuous flow systems using informed prediction models based on low-cost surrogate nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05761)
*Kai Dahms,Eilien Heinrich,Jochen Schmid,Michael Bortz,Iryna Savych,Regina Bleul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于形状约束的预测建模方法，用于预测微流控制备脂质体和脂质纳米粒的关键特性（尺寸和多分散指数），以减少纳米治疗药物开发中依赖经验性实验筛选的成本和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米治疗药物的开发中，纳米颗粒特性（如尺寸和PDI）对工艺参数高度敏感，缺乏预测性数学模型导致必须依赖大量迭代实验筛选，增加了开发成本和时间。

Method: 采用形状约束建模方法，结合实验数据与专家知识，在受控微流控条件下系统制备脂质体和脂质纳米粒，改变脂质浓度、流速和水相-有机相混合比例等参数，并在药物应用场景下用最少经验数据验证模型。

Result: 形状约束模型能够准确预测纳米颗粒的尺寸和分散性，显著减少了对大量实验流程的依赖，仅需最少经验数据即可在药物应用中完成验证。

Conclusion: 该框架支持纳米药物制造工艺的理性和高效开发，为减少经验性筛选、降低开发成本提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The development of nanotherapeutics often involves extensive empirical optimization due to the sensitivity of nanoparticle properties, such as size and polydispersity index (PDI), to minor changes in process parameters. Factors like formulation concentration, flow rates, and mixing ratios can significantly influence clinical efficacy and therapeutic outcomes. The absence of predictive mathematical frameworks has made iterative experimental screening necessary, increasing both costs and development time.
  This study introduces and validates a predictive modeling approach based on shape constraints, aiming to enhance the estimation of nanoparticle characteristics across various process conditions. Using controlled microfluidic methods, liposomes and lipid nanoparticles were systematically prepared under varying lipid concentrations, flow rates, and aqueous-to-organic mixing ratios.
  The shape-constrained model, informed by both experimental data and expert knowledge, was subsequently validated for a pharmaceutical application using minimal empirical data. Results reveal that shape-constrained modeling facilitates accurate prediction of nanoparticle size and dispersity, reducing the need for extensive experimental workflows. This framework supports rational and efficient process development for manufacturing nanomedicine systems.

</details>


### [94] [Neuro-Symbolic Closed-Loop Control of Laser Powder Bed Fusion with an In-Loop Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05773)
*Gisuk Hong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于激光粉末床熔融的几何条件化神经符号闭环架构，将符合标准的本体嵌入控制回路，结合符号推理与统计学习为约束感知预测控制器设定目标。通过描述逻辑推理器将工艺目标与约束映射为每条扫描线的参考值及边界，解决了悬垂挂渣（dross）这一无法在制造过程中直接测量的质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在激光粉末床熔融过程中，某些关键质量指标（如熔池深度）无法在线直接测量，而现有几何盲控制器因缺乏几何上下文信息会产生挂渣等缺陷。需要一种能够将不可观测的质量目标映射为可观测信号约束，并动态调整控制目标的方法。

Method: 构建符合标准的本体，在控制回路中将工艺目标与约束链接到控制器可观测的信号；描述逻辑推理器将目标转换为每条扫描线的参考值与边界。针对悬垂挂渣，利用几何与功率相关的深度-宽度比（由高斯过程提供标定比率及校准不确定性）将熔池深度约束映射为可观测宽度约束。推理器对每个特征分类并选择活动约束（悬垂处增加未熔合下限、标定范围外增加单调守卫、工艺窗口内增加能量密度上限），仅在几何上下文变化时触发推理，平时保留单个小规模二次规划问题在每条扫描路径上运行。

Result: 在标定至NIST AM-Bench IN625基准的Eagar-Tsai替代模型中，该架构消除了几何盲控制器产生的挂渣，在双重评分下保持零挂渣仅存少量未熔合残留，在故意引入的不匹配下优雅退化，且更换合金与约束只需编辑本体数据而非修改代码。

Conclusion: 研究确立了该神经符号闭环架构的可行性，实验标定深度-宽度比是下一步的主要工作。

Abstract: A geometry-conditioned, neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture is proposed for laser powder bed fusion, in which a standards-aligned ontology operates inside the control loop and couples symbolic reasoning with statistical learning to set the targets of a constraint-aware predictive controller. The ontology links the process objectives and constraints to the signals a controller can observe, and a description-logic reasoner converts them into the references and bounds enforced on each scan. The demonstrated case is overhang dross, a quality limit on the melt pool depth, which governs quality yet cannot be measured during the build, is mapped through a geometry- and power-dependent depth-to-width ratio onto a bound on the observable width, with the ratio and its calibrated uncertainty supplied by a Gaussian process. The reasoner classifies each upcoming feature and selects the active constraints-adding a lack-of-fusion floor at overhangs, a monotone guard beyond the calibrated range, and an energy-density cap where a process window is declared while running only on changes of geometric context and otherwise leaving a single small quadratic program on the per-scan path. In an Eagar-Tsai surrogate calibrated to the NIST AM-Bench benchmark for IN625, the architecture eliminates the dross produced by a geometry-blind controller, holds dross at zero with only a small residual lack-of-fusion under dual scoring, degrades gracefully under deliberate plant mismatch, and retargets to new alloys and constraints by editing ontology data rather than code. The results establish architectural feasibility, experimental calibration of the ratio is the principal next step.

</details>


### [95] [GROM: Gradient-Free Rapid One-Shot Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783)
*Paweł Batorski,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GROM，一种一次性（one-shot）机器遗忘方法，通过将遗忘过程建模为岭正则化最小二乘优化问题，推导出权重矩阵的闭合形式加性更新，无需迭代优化或反向传播，仅依靠前向传播即可在数秒内完成遗忘操作，在遗忘-效用权衡上达到最先进水平，并能抵御低比特量化攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法主要依赖基于微调的迭代式训练，即使使用LoRA等参数高效降维技术仍计算昂贵且缺乏解析公式；梯度优化可能只是将目标知识隐藏而非真正删除，导致量化攻击可恢复被遗忘的内容。因此需要一种快速、精确且真正从权重中移除知识的遗忘方法。

Method: GROM将机器遗忘构建为岭正则化最小二乘（ridge-regularized least-squares）优化问题，对目标权重矩阵推导出闭合形式的加性更新。该更新强制选定层抑制不需要的内容，同时严格保持对保留数据的原有行为。计算仅依赖梯度无关的前向传播，不需要反向传播，也不需要迭代到收敛。

Result: 在TOFU-5%、TOFU-10%、MUSE-Books、MUSE-News和WMDP基准上，GROM实现了最先进的遗忘-效用权衡，相比传统微调方法计算开销降低数个数量级，同时不牺牲整体模型性能。GROM还经受住了低比特量化攻击，而基于梯度的基线方法在这种攻击下会恢复大量已遗忘内容。

Conclusion: GROM证明了通过解析闭式解的one-shot遗忘可以取代迭代优化，不仅大幅降低计算成本，还能从根本上从权重中移除目标知识而非仅隐藏，提升了对量化攻击的鲁棒性，为LLM机器遗忘提供了更高效且更可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become a critical capability for safely removing specific, sensitive knowledge from large language models (LLMs). Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden rather than removed, to the point that simply quantizing the unlearned model restores much of what it was supposed to have erased. To resolve this, we propose a novel one-shot unlearning approach, abandoning iterative optimization in favor of a direct, exact analytical solution. We frame the unlearning process as a ridge-regularized least-squares optimization problem, deriving a closed-form additive update for targeted weight matrices. This update forces the selected layer to suppress unwanted content while strictly preserving its behavior on retained data. Computed from gradient-free forward passes alone, with no backpropagation and no iteration to convergence, GROM applies the weight edit in mere seconds, which makes it orders of magnitude faster than traditional fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that GROM achieves state-of-the-art forgetting-utility trade-offs on TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News and WMDP, significantly reducing computational overhead without sacrificing overall model performance. Because the update removes the targeted content from the weights instead of masking it, GROM also withstands the low-bit quantization attack that recovers much of the content a gradient-based baseline had appeared to forget. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/GROM.

</details>


### [96] [Predicting Task Difficulty Without Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05797)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在无需执行模拟试验（rollouts）的情况下，从事前（ex ante）任务描述中预测智能体任务的难度，涵盖17个智能体基准测试（编码、数学、机器学习、网页导航、函数调用等领域），并提出基于token级熵的预测信号，同时揭示AUC指标在难度估计中的缺陷，以及预期难度与实际难度之间的残差可暴露环境缺陷（如数据污染和不可行任务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务难度决定智能体的成功可能性，而无需耗时的模拟试验即可预先估算难度，将极大帮助环境设计者校准评估基准和构建渐进式训练课程。在长时域（long-horizon）任务中，试错法（trial-and-error）成为严重计算瓶颈，而现有早期预测工作局限于静态任务或隔离的编码环境，依赖狭隘的特征和不准确的评估指标，缺乏跨领域的系统研究。

Method: 作者在17个智能体基准测试中系统研究ex ante难度预测，涉及编码、数学、机器学习、网页导航、函数调用等多个领域。具体包括：1）分析AUC作为评估指标在难度预测中的局限性；2）提出token级熵（token-level entropy）作为有效的难度预测信号；3）通过预期难度与实际观察难度之间的残差分析，检测隐藏的环境缺陷（如数据污染和任务不可行性）。

Result: 研究发现AUC可能掩盖较差的难度估计质量；token级熵被确认为有用的预测信号；预期与实际难度之间的残差能够有效暴露环境中的潜在缺陷，包括数据污染（contamination）和任务不可行（infeasibility）等问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨领域的智能体任务难度事前预测提供了系统性研究方法，证明了简单可解释的文本特征（如token熵）在难度估算中的有效性，并提出残差分析作为诊断基准环境质量的新工具，对评估基准设计和训练课程构建具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Task difficulty dictates an agent's likelihood of success, and estimating it without rollouts means forecasting this directly from a task description before executing costly simulations in stateful environments. Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottleneck. Prior work on early prediction is limited to static tasks or isolated coding environments, often relying on narrow features and inaccurate evaluation metrics. We study \textit{ex ante} difficulty prediction across 17 agentic benchmarks spanning coding, mathematics, machine learning, web navigation, function calling, and other domains. We show that AUC can mask poor difficulty estimates, identify token-level entropy as a useful predictive signal, and show how residuals between expected and observed difficulty can expose hidden environment flaws such as contamination and infeasibility.

</details>


### [97] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于学习排序（Learning-to-Rank）的框架，用于在GPU上执行张量网络收缩之前选择高效的收缩计划。通过从成对收缩序列中提取结构特征，训练梯度提升排序器（使用listwise和pairwise目标），模型能在多个电路族上选出优于随机和MinFill基线的收缩计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟对量子算法的开发与验证至关重要，但其成本随电路规模快速增长。张量网络收缩可以通过利用电路结构来降低成本，但所选收缩计划的效率高度依赖其选择方案。在GPU上，理论复杂度相近的计划实际性能差异可能很大，因为执行还取决于并行度、归约结构、内存流量和收缩几何形状。因此需要一种能在执行前高效选择收缩计划的方法。

Method: 提出学习排序框架：为每个收缩计划从其序列化的成对收缩中提取结构特征，在GPU测量数据上使用梯度提升模型训练排序器，分别训练listwise和pairwise两种目标的模型；评估时使用分布内测试集和电路族偏移测试集，并对比随机选择与MinFill基线；此外还通过两种GPU架构上的性能比较来研究后端迁移能力。

Result: 学习到的排序模型总体上能识别出更好的收缩计划，其中listwise模型提供了最强的整体决策质量。在不同GPU之间，计划的排序基本稳定但并非完全一致，源设备上训练的模型在目标设备上无需重训仍能保持有用的决策质量。

Conclusion: Learning-to-Rank是一种实用的收缩计划搜索缩减方法，可以有效降低搜索成本并选择更优计划，同时表明计划性能仍然部分依赖于后端硬件特性。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [98] [Evidential Rule Learning for Interpretable Classification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05859)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERL是一种可解释的快速证据规则学习方法，直接从前向模糊隶属度中产生信念、似然和弃权能力，无需辅助头或事后校准，在30个表格数据集上精度显著优于现有规则学习器，并在近OOD检测和概念瓶颈评估中达到或超越专用检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释分类在实际部署中不仅需要准确预测，还需要透明地展示决策依据，并在无法可靠决策时主动弃权。现有方法通常依赖事后校准或辅助结构来提供不确定性估计，缺乏天然的证据语义，难以同时保证可解释性、准确性和可靠性。
