### [156] [The prediction of extreme uncertainty-production events in three-dimensional Navier-Stokes turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05208)
*Jin Ge,Joran Rolland,John Christos Vassilicos*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维Navier-Stokes湍流中不确定性能量的指数增长，重点分析不确定性产生项的间歇性与高度局部化放大特征。通过在Q-R平面（速度梯度张量第二、第三不变量）中考察应变率、涡量和涡变形等关键场，并结合DNS数据在不同雷诺数下估计committor函数，确认应变率是驱动不确定性的关键因素，且当应变率与涡量接近其空间平均值时，仅基于这些场的概率预测无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流系统的可预测性是流体力学中的核心挑战。不确定性在湍流中如何增长及其局部放大机制直接决定了预测的可行性与可靠性，因此需要识别控制不确定性产生项的关键动力学场并量化其极端事件的概率。

Method: 从Navier-Stokes方程推导出影响不确定性产生项（P_Δ）增长/衰减的关键场（应变率、涡量、涡变形），在Q-R相平面中分析这些场的动力学行为；利用周期域中不同雷诺数的直接数值模拟（DNS）数据，在整个时空域上估计committor函数，获得局部不确定性产生项罕见极端事件的概率估计。

Result: 概率估计结果表明应变率在驱动不确定性中起主导作用；当应变率和涡量过于接近各自的空间平均值时，仅基于所考虑的场无法实现稳定的概率预测。

Conclusion: 研究确认了应变率对不确定性增长的核心驱动作用，并指出在某些场状态（贴近空间均值）下，仅依赖现有场信息进行可预测性估计存在根本性局限，为湍流可预测性研究提供了新的概率框架和边界条件。

Abstract: We investigate the exponential growth of uncertainty energy in 3D Navier-Stokes turbulence, emphasising the intermittent and highly localized amplification/production of uncertainty, a critical factor in understanding the predictability of turbulent systems. From the Navier-Stokes equations one can identify some key fields contributing to the growth/decay of uncertainty-production term $P_Δ$: strain rate, vorticity, and vortex deformation. The dynamics of these fields are examined in the $Q-R$ plane, where $Q$ and $R$ are the second and third invariants of the velocity gradient tensor, to understand their role in the evolution of uncertainty-production term $P_Δ$. We proceed by estimating committor functions across the entire spatiotemporal domain of direct numerical simulations (DNS) of turbulence in a periodic domain at different Reynolds numbers. Our estimates of the probability of rare extreme events of local uncertainty-production term as a function of uncertainty energy, strain rate, vorticity, and vortex deformation confirm the role of strain rate in driving uncertainty. Where strain rate and vorticity are too close to their space-average values, stable probabilistic forecasts appear impossible solely on the basis of the fields considered here.

</details>


### [157] [Confinement-induced evolution and breakup of viscoelastic filaments in microfluidic coflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05343)
*U. K. Kar,T. Sujith,D. Ghosh,A. K. Sen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了矩形微通道中剪切变稀粘弹性液体与不相溶牛顿流体共流时的液丝断裂行为，识别出四种流动制度（稳定共流、挤压、滴流、射流），发现弹性虽然对主液滴形成影响微弱，但通过延迟毛细断裂显著延长液丝寿命。通过标度分析和Oldroyd-B模型，建立了预测主液滴尺寸、临界液丝厚度、最大液丝长度及射流长度的框架，并揭示壁面剪切-弹性耦合机制是控制受限粘弹性断裂的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典粘弹性液丝变薄由弹性毛细动力学描述，但在受限微通道中壁面诱导剪切、弹性和毛细作用的耦合效应仍不清楚，缺乏系统的实验表征和预测框架。

Method: 在矩形微通道中开展剪切变稀粘弹性液体与不可溶牛顿流体共流的实验研究，通过改变两相毛细数和弹性毛细参数识别流动制度；结合粒子追踪测速表征界面速度场；运用基于毛细力、粘性力和弹性力平衡的标度分析预测关键参数；采用考虑Oldroyd-B模型有效粘度的Rayleigh-Plateau分析预测不稳定性波长和增长率。

Result: 识别出四种流动制度（稳定共流、挤压、滴流、射流），并绘制了相图。弹性对主液滴形成影响微弱，但显著延迟毛细断裂并稳定长寿命液丝。建立标度模型预测主液滴尺寸、临界液丝厚度、最大液丝长度和临界射流长度。发现受限几何产生非均匀壁面剪切场，界面速度的空间变化在最大剪切位置触发首个bead-on-a-string不稳定性。Rayleigh-Plateau分析成功预测不稳定波长和增长率至正确量级。

Conclusion: 受限粘弹性断裂并非仅由经典弹性毛细变薄主导，而是由壁面剪切-弹性耦合机制控制液丝拉伸、不稳定性和次级液滴形成，为受限粘弹性多相流中的液丝介导断裂提供了预测框架。

Abstract: Viscoelastic filament thinning is classically described by elastocapillary dynamics in extensional flows, yet in confined microchannels the combined effects of wall-induced shear, elasticity, and capillarity remain poorly understood. Here, we experimentally investigate the breakup of a shear-thinning viscoelastic liquid coflowing with an immiscible Newtonian fluid in a rectangular microchannel, focusing on the formation, stretching, instability, and breakup of the thin filament connecting the primary droplet to the upstream liquid. Four regimes: stable coflow, squeezing, dripping, and jetting are identified and mapped using the capillary numbers of the dispersed and continuous phases and an elastocapillary parameter. Although elasticity weakly affects the onset of primary droplet formation, it strongly alters later filament dynamics by delaying capillary breakup and stabilising long-lived filaments. Scaling analyses based on capillary, viscous, and elastic force balances predict the primary droplet size, critical filament thickness at instability onset, maximum filament length, and critical jet length. Particle tracking shows that confinement creates a non-uniform wall-induced shear field along the inclined filament, producing spatial variations in interfacial velocity and initiating the first bead-on-a-string instability at the location of maximum shear. A Rayleigh-Plateau analysis incorporating an effective viscosity derived from the Oldroyd-B model predicts the instability wavelength and growth rate to the correct order of magnitude. These results show that confined viscoelastic breakup is governed not solely by classical elastocapillary thinning, but by a coupled wall-shear-elasticity mechanism controlling filament stretching, instability, and secondary droplet formation, thereby providing a predictive framework for filament-mediated breakup in confined viscoelastic multiphase flows.

</details>


### [158] [Effect of Discharge on the Nonlinear Bar Growth: A Flume Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05593)
*Shohei Seki,Daichi Moteki,Hiroyasu Yasuda*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过流式层析成像技术（Stream Tomography）在16种流量与坡度组合条件下连续观测交替沙坝的演化，发现坝高呈S形增长并可由Landau方程描述。结果显示无量纲生长率随流量减小而增大，但敏感性弱于单模态稳定性理论预测，其归因于多模态相互作用和线性化限制；低流量条件下坝高-水深比更大导致水流偏转、输沙受限、冲刷增强，从而促进坝高增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替沙坝是冲积河流中普遍存在的地貌形态，坝高过度增长会增加洪水风险和破坏生态系统。预测坝高变化需要量化坝高生长率对水流流量的依赖关系，但传统水槽实验会中断水流，无法连续捕捉沙坝演化过程，因此此前鲜有研究实现这一目标。

Method: 在四种流量和四种坡度共16种工况下开展实验室实验，采用非侵入式、可流通的Stream Tomography技术连续测绘河床地形；利用Landau方程拟合坝高的S形生长曲线以估计生长率和平衡坝高；并通过数值水流模拟分析坝高-水深比与水流偏转、泥沙输移之间的关系。

Result: 坝高呈S形生长且可由Landau方程良好描述；无量纲生长率随流量降低而增大，但其对流量的敏感性弱于单模态稳定性理论的预测，且在低流量下偏差更明显。数值模拟表明低流量下坝高-水深比更大，水流更易偏转，淤积区下游泥沙输移受限、坝迁移被抑制，水流和泥沙输送集中于冲刷区，加剧局部冲刷并促进坝高增长。

Conclusion: 该研究为沙坝稳定性理论提供了基准约束，有助于改进坝高对流量变化响应的预测能力。

Abstract: Alternate bars are ubiquitous bedforms in alluvial rivers, and excessive bar-height growth can increase flood risk and disrupt ecosystems. Predicting bar-height variability requires quantifying the dependence of bar growth rate on water discharge, yet this has rarely been achieved because conventional flume measurements interrupt the flow and cannot continuously capture bar evolution. Here we conducted laboratory experiments under four discharges and four channel slopes (16 conditions) and continuously mapped bed topography using Stream Tomography, a nonintrusive, flow-through measurement technique. Bar height exhibits sigmoidal growth and is well described by the Landau equation, allowing robust estimation of the growth rate and equilibrium height. The inferred dimensionless growth rate tends to increase as discharge decreases, but its sensitivity is weaker than that predicted by stability theory for a single alternate-bar mode. This discrepancy becomes more pronounced at lower discharge, indicating limitations of linearization and the influence of interactions among multiple bar modes. Furthermore, numerical flow simulations over the measured bed topography reveal that, during bar development, lower-discharge conditions produce a relatively larger bar height-to-depth ratio, making the flow more prone to deflection. In such cases, sediment transport is restricted downstream of depositional areas, thereby limiting bar migration. Consequently, flow and sediment transport become concentrated in the scour zones, enhancing local scour and promoting the growth of bar height. These results provide benchmark constraints for bar stability theories and help improve predictions of how bar height responds to changes in discharge.

</details>


### [159] [Area and Perimeter Rules of Velocity Circulation in Two-Dimensional Turbulence with Large-scale Absolute Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05617)
*Zi-Ang Zhang,Jin-Han Xie*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文证明了速度环量的面积规则在二维均匀各向同性湍流的大尺度绝对平衡态（满足涡量等分布）中严格成立，并推导出新的周长规则（环量PDF仅依赖回路周长而非面积），在能量等分布的绝对平衡态中严格成立。两种规则由特征平衡尺度l_eq分隔，且统计规律在极低纵横比（0.03）的回路中依然保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，速度环量的面积规则被认为与湍流惯性区相关，但已证明并非精确成立。研究者希望探索在二维湍流绝对平衡态这一新设定下，是否存在严格满足的面积规则，并寻找新的几何统计不变量。

Method: 通过二维无粘涡量回路方程（loop equation）进行理论推导，在两种绝对平衡态（涡量等分布与能量等分布）下分别验证面积规则与周长规则，并分析介于两者之间由特征尺度l_eq划分的中间状态下的统计行为。

Result: 发现面积规则在涡量等分布的绝对平衡态中严格成立；新推导的周长规则在能量等分布的绝对平衡态中严格成立；两个区域由平衡尺度l_eq分隔（l<<l_eq时用面积规则，l>>l_eq时用周长规则）；统计规律在纵横比低至0.03的极端回路中仍然稳健，远超惯性区研究的极限。

Conclusion: 本研究为二维loop equation提供了新的稳态解并暗示了更多解的存在，为发现湍流中先前未知的几何不变量铺平了道路。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the area rule of velocity circulation -- traditionally associated with the turbulent inertial range but shown not to be exact -- is strictly satisfied in the large-scale absolute equilibrium of two-dimensional (2D) homogeneous isotropic turbulence under enstrophy equipartition. We also derive a novel perimeter rule from the 2D inviscid loop equation, which posits that the probability distribution function (PDF) of velocity circulation depends solely on the loop perimeter rather than its area. This perimeter rule holds strictly in the large-scale absolute equilibrium characterized by energy equipartition. At the intermediate states determined by both enstrophy and energy, these two regimes are separated by a characteristic equilibrium scale $l_\text{eq}$: the area rule governs loop statistics when $l\ll l_\text{eq}$, while the perimeter rule emerges for $l\gg l_\text{eq}$. These statistical laws remain robust even for loops with extreme aspect ratios as low as $0.03$, a value that inertial-range studies never achieved. Our findings provide a new steady-state solution to the 2D loop equation and suggest additional solutions, paving the way for exploring previously undiscovered geometric invariants of turbulence.

</details>


### [160] [Onsager-variational-principle-based Lattice Boltzmann Model For Three-phase Dielectric Fluid Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05787)
*Xinyue Liu,Xu Qian,Fang Xiong,Lei Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Onsager变分原理的热力学一致三相电液动力学（EHD）模型，并开发了介观格子玻尔兹曼方法进行数值求解，通过多个基准算例验证后，研究了电场下双液滴聚并分离及剪切流耦合等复杂动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三相EHD数值研究多依赖现象学模型，忽视热力学一致性和关键的表面电荷对流机制，导致无法准确捕捉复杂多物理场相互作用，需要建立更严格的物理模型。

Method: 从Onsager变分原理严格推导热力学一致的三相EHD模型，无需先验假设即可内在保证热力学一致性；随后开发介观格子玻尔兹曼方法求解该模型，自然捕捉界面演化和电荷输运。

Result: 通过电渗流、三相液透镜铺展、静态复合液滴平衡及电场下复合液滴变形等基准算例验证了数值框架的准确性，并成功模拟了电场下双液滴聚并分离以及EHD与剪切流耦合下液滴的复杂动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个稳健且热力学可靠的数值工具，适用于探索多相EHD系统的高度非线性行为，弥补了现有研究在热力学一致性和表面电荷对流机制方面的不足。

Abstract: Multiphase electrohydrodynamic (EHD) flows play a crucial role in various engineering applications. However, existing numerical studies on three-phase electrohydrodynamic systems predominantly rely on phenomenological models, often neglecting thermodynamic consistency and critical surface charge convection mechanisms. To address these fundamental gaps, this paper proposes a thermodynamically consistent three-phase EHD model derived strictly from the Onsager variational principle. This theoretical framework intrinsically guarantees thermodynamic consistency and accurately captures complex multiphysics interactions without requiring a priori assumptions. Furthermore, a mesoscopic lattice Boltzmann method is developed to solve the proposed model, enabling the natural capture of interfacial evolution and charge transport. The accuracy of the numerical framework are rigorously validated against several benchmark cases, including electroosmotic flow in microchannels, the spreading of a three-phase liquid lens, the equilibrium of static compound droplet, and the deformation of compound droplet under uniform electric field. Using this validated framework, we investigate EHD applications, specifically simulating the complex dynamics of double droplet coalescence and separation under electric field, as well as the behavior of droplets subjected to combined EHD and shear flow. Overall, this work provides a robust, thermodynamically reliable numerical tool for exploring the highly nonlinear behaviors of multiphase EHD systems.

</details>


### [161] [Three-layer water flows: Dirichlet-Neumann operators and approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06314)
*Rossen Ivanov,Calin-Iulian Martin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有分段恒定密度分层的三层不可压无粘流体（平底部、自由表面和两个界面）的非线性运动方程，建立了基于Dirichlet-Neumann算子的哈密顿体系，导出了线性色散关系的双三次方程及其解的解析分析，并给出了传播速度的上下界和近似公式，同时分析了刚性盖子（平坦表面）下弱非线性传播（Boussinesq近似）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三层分层流体（含自由表面和双界面）的内波运动是海洋学与湖泊学的重要问题，但其非线性哈密顿结构此前尚未被系统地建立。建立该框架可为后续线性分析与弱非线性分析提供统一的数学工具，并拓展到实际海洋与湖泊内波以及三层实验室实验的应用。

Method: 为每一层引入Dirichlet-Neumann（DN）算子，将系统的哈密顿量及表/界面运动方程用DN算子表示；通过小振幅展开在线性阶导出色散关系（双三次方程），分析其六个实根并给出上下界；对DN算子进行适当展开以获得弱非线性Boussinesq近似；并通过退化极限恢复两层自由表面模型。

Result: 获得了三层流体的哈密顿公式化表示；色散关系为双三次方程，其六个实根（三个右传波与三个左传波）速度量级不同，且给出了根的上界和下界及近似传播速度公式；在刚性盖子情况下同样获得了基于DN算子的演化方程、线性方程和Boussinesq近似方程；两层自由表面模型作为极限情形被恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作为三层分层流体中的内波研究提供了完整的哈密顿框架，结果适用于湖泊、海洋中的内波问题以及三层叠加流体的实验室实验，具有良好的理论普适性和应用前景。

Abstract: The object of investigation in this paper are the nonlinear equations of motion for two-dimensional inviscid water flows with piecewise constant density stratification in a three-layer fluid with a flat bottom, a free surface and two interfaces. We establish a Hamiltonian formulation for the nonlinear governing equations in this setup. The Hamiltonian of the system and the equations of motion of the surface and of the interfaces are expressed with the help of the Dirichlet-Neumann (DN) operators, which are introduced for each of the layers. Then, the linear equations for small amplitudes of the elevation of the surface and of the interfaces in the leading order are derived from which a bi-cubic equation for the dispersion relation is obtained, whose solutions are analysed. The six real solutions for the possible propagation speeds (three positive, related to right-moving waves and three negative, related to left-moving waves) have magnitudes of different order. Upper and lower bounds for the previously mentioned roots are also given in terms of the coefficients of the equation. Subsequently, approximate formulae for the propagation speeds are derived. The importance of the DN operators is further illustrated in a separate analysis of the three-layer model with flat surface (rigid lid). The full nonlinear evolution equations are expressed again in terms of the DN operators, and the equations in the linear regime and the weakly nonlinear propagation regime (the Boussinesq approximation) are derived by a proper expansion of the DN operators. Limits to the two-layer free surface model are obtained as well. The obtained results are applicable to internal waves in lakes and in the ocean as well as to laboratory experiments with three superimposed fluid layers.

</details>


### [162] [Drop Spray Electrification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05828)
*Riko Kazama,Masaya Toda,Hans-Jürgen Butt,Rutvik Lathia*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了水滴撞击超疏水网格时，液体穿透并破碎通过网格孔隙会产生带电次级液滴。通过法拉第杯电荷测量与高速成像相结合，作者量化了电荷随韦伯数的变化规律，发现低韦伯数下回缩射流产生的比电荷最高，高韦伯数下煎饼反弹抑制射流形成使得电荷趋于饱和；更小孔隙和导电网格分别增强电荷质量比和确保重复冲击下电荷稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管水滴破碎过程中的电荷分离现象（如瀑布和喷雾带电）早已被认识，但控制性液滴破碎在结构化疏液表面上的带电机制仍不清楚，缺乏对韦伯数和喷雾形成路径对电荷影响的系统理解，限制了相关平台的设计。

Method: 将水滴撞击超疏水网格，使液体穿透并在网格孔隙间破碎形成次级带电液滴；采用法拉第杯电荷测量结合高速成像技术，系统考察电荷与韦伯数的依赖关系，并分别分析不同喷雾形成路径（回缩射流与冲击诱导射流）的贡献；同时对比不同网格孔隙尺寸与导电性能的影响。

Result: 穿透阈值以下无电荷可测；低韦伯数时回缩射流产生高的单位喷雾质量电荷，中等韦伯数时回缩射流与冲击诱导射流共同贡献带电，高韦伯数时煎饼反弹抑制回缩射流，电荷由冲击诱导穿透主导并趋于饱和值；更小网格孔隙增加电荷质量比，导电网格通过耗散残余表面电荷实现重复冲击下的稳定带电。

Conclusion: 这些发现为超疏水网格平台在喷雾带电和雨驱能量收集方面的设计提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Charge separation during the breakup of water drop has been recognized since the early studies of waterfall and spray electrification, yet the role of controlled drop fragmentation at structured liquid-repellent surfaces remains unclear. Here we show that water drops impacting superhydrophobic meshes generate charged secondary droplets as liquid penetrates and fragments through the mesh pores. By combining Faraday-cup charge measurements with high-speed imaging, we identify how the charge depends on the Weber number and on the pathway of spray formation. No measurable charge is detected below the penetration threshold. At low Weber numbers, recoil jet formation gives a high charge per unit spray mass, whereas at intermediate Weber numbers both recoil jets and impact-induced jets contribute to charging. At higher Weber numbers, pancake bouncing suppresses recoil jet formation, so charging is dominated by impact induced penetration and the total charge approaches a saturated value. We further show that smaller mesh pores enhance the charge-to-mass ratio and that conductive meshes provide stable charging under repeated impacts by dissipating residual surface charge. These findings provide design principles for superhydrophobic mesh platforms for spray charging and rain-driven energy harvesting.

</details>


### [163] [Magnetic Dynamo Driven by Inertial Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06086)
*A. Mishra,G. Mamatsashvili,M. Le Bars,A. J. Barker,F. Stefani*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过研究进动驱动的流动，证明了惯性波流体动力学湍流可以驱动稳健的磁发电机作用。通过对大尺度地转涡施加受控阻尼，即使在极低的磁Prandtl数（Pm~10⁻³）和Poincaré数（Po~0.025）下，惯性波发电机的增长率也显著提高。该结果确立了惯性波作为快速旋转天体内部低Pm流动中磁场放大的有效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在天体物理和行星科学中，快速旋转的流体内部（如行星和恒星内部）磁场是如何产生和维持的仍然是一个重要问题。惯性波湍流被认为可能参与磁发电机过程，但以往研究中伴生的大尺度地转涡会耗散波能量并抑制发电机效应，因此需要探索控制这些涡旋以揭示惯性波本身的发电机潜力。

Method: 通过研究进动驱动的流动，采用数值模拟方法分析惯性波流体动力学湍流中的发电机行为。方法上引入对伴生大尺度地转涡的可控阻尼项，在运动学（kinematic）框架下考察不同涡旋阻尼强度、磁Prandtl数（Pm）和Poincaré数（Po）下发电机的增长率变化，并结合动力学螺旋度的相干涨落和谱分析来揭示磁场增长的机制。

Result: 研究发现，即使很小的涡旋阻尼也能显著提高惯性波发电机的增长率，使发电机在Pm低至~10⁻³、Po低至~0.025时仍能维持。发电机的临界Po和Pm随Reynolds数增大而降低。发电机启动和增长与动力学螺旋度的相干涨落相关，谱分析表明磁场能量增长主要来源于惯性波诱导的感应效应，覆盖广泛的尺度范围。

Conclusion: 惯性波被确立为快速旋转低Pm流动中磁场放大的高效机制，对理解行星和恒星内部的磁场产生具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: We demonstrate, by studying precession-driven flows, that inertial wave hydrodynamic turbulence can drive a robust magnetic dynamo action. Motivated by the stronger damping of large-scale geostrophic vortices in rapidly rotating planetary and stellar interiors, we introduce a controlled damping of the vortices, which usually accompany inertial wave turbulence and feed on wave energy. It is shown that even a small vortex damping results in a significant increase of the growth rate of the dynamo due to inertial waves in the kinematic regime, allowing it to persist for magnetic Prandtl numbers as low as $Pm \sim 10^{-3}$ and Poincaré numbers $Po\sim 0.025$. These critical values of $Po$ and $Pm$ for the dynamo onset decrease with increasing Reynolds number. The onset and growth of the dynamo appear to correlate with the coherent fluctuations of kinetic helicity. Spectral analysis shows that magnetic energy growth is primarily due to inertial-wave-induced induction over a broad range of scales. These results establish inertial waves as an efficient mechanism for magnetic field amplification in rapidly rotating low-$Pm$ flows relevant to planetary and stellar interiors.

</details>


### [164] [Wave scattering around a submerged vertical permeable breakwater](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06089)
*Jeongin Kim,Yong Sung Park*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过摄动法获得了无限水深条件下淹没式可渗透垂直板式防波堤在单色线性波作用下的波浪速度场解析解，并通过Riemann-Hilbert问题求解了各阶速度势，同时推导了能量恒等式以验证一阶解并给出展开有效范围的闭合边界表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可渗透垂直板式防波堤在港口和海岸工程中具有重要应用价值，但现有解析解多针对不可渗透或有限水深情况。本文旨在为无限水深下淹没式可渗透垂直板防波堤提供解析解，并讨论波长、板长和渗透率等参数对反射和透射系数的影响，以填补该领域的空白。

Method: 采用二维势流理论，将不可压缩、无粘、无旋流动下的非线性边界条件通过以小参数（表征渗透率）展开的摄动法求解。解展开至一阶：零阶项代表不可渗透防波堤的波浪散射，一阶项给出可渗透修正。各阶速度势通过约化方法求解，分别归结为齐次和非齐次Riemann-Hilbert问题。

Result: 获得了波浪速度场的一阶解析解，详细讨论了波长、防波堤长度和渗透率条件对反射系数与透射系数的影响。推导出精确的能量恒等式，该恒等式验证了一阶解，并给出了展开有效范围的闭合形式边界ε_max(kb)。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了无限水深下淹没式可渗透垂直板防波堤的解析求解框架，一阶摄动解结合Riemann-Hilbert方法有效解决了渗透性边界条件的非线性问题，能量恒等式的导出为解的验证和适用范围界定提供了严格的数学依据。

Abstract: An analytical solution for a wave velocity field scattered by a submerged permeable vertical plate-type breakwater under the linear monochromatic wave is obtained and the applications of the solution are presented. The water has an infinite depth, and the flow is assumed to be incompressible, inviscid, and irrotational, which leads to the two-dimensional potential wave theory. The permeable breakwater vertically occupies a finite interval beneath the water surface and the water flows through the breakwater. The resulting nonlinear boundary condition is resolved by the perturbation method with a small parameter representing the permeability. The solution was expanded up to the first order so that the leading-order term can represent the wave scattered by the impermeable breakwater and the first-order term can give the correction to the solution considering the wave scattered by the permeable breakwater. Each order of the wave velocity potential is determined by a reduction method and this leads to the homogeneous Riemann-Hilbert problem for the leading-order problem and the nonhomogeneous Riemann-Hilbert problem for the first-order problem. \rev{The effects} of wavelength, breakwater length, and breakwater permeability conditions on the reflection and transmission coefficients are discussed in detail as an illustrative example of the application of the solution. \rev{An exact energy identity is also derived; it verifies the first-order solution and yields a closed-form boundary $\varepsilon_{\max}(kb)$ of the validity range of the expansion.

</details>


### [165] [From expansion to collapse: Bubble and continuum multiscale modeling in open-system magmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06181)
*Janine Birnbaum,Fabian B. Wadsworth,Anthony Lamur,Jackie E. Kendrick,Yan Lavallee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多尺度耦合模型（MVFFIN），将微尺度气泡内挥发性物质扩散的力学模型与宏尺度岩浆悬浮体的热演化和流体流动耦合起来，实现气泡生长与收缩的双向反馈模拟，并识别出由黏性阻力、扩散限制、黏性输运、脱气和热淬火主导的多个动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅酸盐熔体中气泡生长驱动显著的体积膨胀，对岩浆运移动力学具有一阶控制作用。当岩浆暴露于外部环境时，自由表面的热量和挥发性物质损失可逆转气泡生长导致收缩，并产生扩散、流变学和流动之间的复杂反馈。现有模型难以解析这种多尺度、双向耦合的相互作用。

Method: 采用双向耦合方法：将单个气泡内挥发性扩散的微机械模型，与周围岩浆悬浮体的宏观热演化和流体流动模型相耦合。模型捕获气泡大小、熔体黏度和压力梯度的协同演化，使生长和再吸收从局部条件中自然涌现。实现于模块化的数值框架MVFFIN中，具有灵活可扩展性。

Result: 识别出多个不同的动力学机制，包括：(i) 气泡生长受黏性阻力或气泡尺度扩散限制，(ii) 悬浮体的黏性输运，(iii) 通过渗透性多孔网络和暴露岩浆-流体界面的脱气，(iv) 热淬火。发现由温度和挥发性浓度梯度形成的薄的高黏度边界层在调节流动和气泡演化中起核心作用。

Conclusion: 通过解析内部气泡动力学与外部边界条件之间的相互作用，该模型为理解多尺度脱气及其对岩浆输运和破碎的影响提供了统一框架，可扩展应用于管道流动和火山碎屑演化等广泛场景。

Abstract: Bubble growth in silicate melts drives significant volume expansion, which has a first order control on magma transport dynamics. When magmas are exposed to external environments, heat and volatile loss at free surfaces can reverse bubble growth, leading to shrinkage and complex feedbacks between diffusion, rheology, and flow. To resolve how magma flow controls, or is controlled by, bubble expansion, we couple a micro-mechanical model for volatile diffusion into individual bubbles, with a macro-scale thermal evolution and fluid flow of the surrounding magmatic suspension. This two-way coupling captures the co-evolution of bubble size, melt viscosity, and pressure gradients, allowing both growth and resorption to emerge naturally from local conditions. We identify distinct dynamical regimes governed by (i) bubble growth limited by (a) viscous resistance or (b) diffusion at the bubble scale, (ii) viscous transport of the suspension, (iii) outgassing through permeable porous networks and exposed magma-fluid interfaces, and (iv) thermal quenching. Across these regimes, thin, high-viscosity boundary layers arising from temperature and volatile concentration gradients play a central role in modulating flow and bubble evolution. The model is implemented in a flexible, modular numerical framework (Multiscale Vesiculation, Fluid flow, Failure, and Interaction Nonlinear model: MVFFIN) enabling extension to a wide range of systems and applications, including conduit flow and pyroclast evolution. By resolving the interplay between internal bubble dynamics and external boundary conditions, this approach provides a unified framework for understanding multiscale degassing and its impact on magmatic transport and fragmentation.

</details>


### [166] [VENUSS: a unified finite-element model of solidifying lava](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06199)
*Janine Birnbaum,Einat Lev,Marc W. Spiegelma,Jackie E. Kendrick,Yan Lavallee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VENUSS（Viscous-Elastic Numerically Unified Solver for Solidifying flows）的新数值模型，用于模拟冷却和凝固的自由表面流动。该模型将粘性流体内部与随冷却增厚的弹性壳层耦合，以更准确地捕捉熔岩流和穹丘表面"硬壳"形成后变形机制从粘性到弹塑性的转变。结果表明，弹性壳层耦合的穹丘状形态会产生更多侧向扩张和更少的垂直隆升，说明在固态层中传递横向应力对于准确解释和预测穹丘变形至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔岩流和熔岩穹丘表面形成固态硬壳后，其变形机制从粘性主导转变为弹性或塑性主导，这种转变显著影响流动的速率、方式及通道化流动、过陡边缘和熔岩破出推进等过程。特别是在水下、冰川下和地外环境中冷却加速，需要针对这些环境专门校准的模型，而现有模型缺乏对固化壳层影响的恰当描述。

Method: 提出VENUSS数值模型，将粘性流体内部与弹性壳层耦合求解。弹性壳层的厚度随冷却过程增长，模型为自由表面流动的冷却与固化问题提供了统一数值求解框架。通过与"高粘性外壳"的对比实验，验证了弹性壳层对流动形态的影响。

Result: 演示结果表明：由底部供料的穹丘状形态在具有弹性壳层并耦合基底地形时，比具有可比较的高粘性外壳时产生更多侧向扩张和更少的垂直抬升。这揭示了固态层中横向应力传递的重要性。

Conclusion: 弹性固态壳层中的横向应力传递是准确解释和预测熔岩穹丘变形的关键因素，仅用高粘性外壳代替弹性壳层无法正确捕捉冷却固化流动的行为。VENUSS模型为这类问题的模拟提供了有效的数值工具。

Abstract: The development of a solid rind or carapace at the surface of lava flows and domes results in a transition in deformation mechanism from dominantly viscous to elastic or plastic. This transition has a significant impact on the rate and style of emplacement, including on the construction of channelized flows, over-steepened margins, and flow advance due to lava breakouts. These processes are particularly important in subaqueous, subglacial, and extraterrestrial environments in which cooling is accelerated, requiring models specifically calibrated for these environments. We present a new numerical model, Viscous-Elastic Numerically Unified Solver for Solidifying flows (VENUSS), for cooling and solidifying free surface flows. The model couples a viscous fluid interior with an elastic shell whose thickness grows in response to cooling. As a demonstration of the impact of including a solidified crust in the flow model, we show that a dome-like shape fed from below with an elastic shell coupled to the basal topography results in more lateral expansion and less vertical uplift than a comparable highly-viscous rind, demonstrating the need for lateral transfer of stress in solid layers to accurately interpret and predict dome deformation.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），用于高效计算参数化线性控制系统在指定时间区间内的输入输出响应，并达到用户指定精度。CIM通过在修正的积分轮廓上应用求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）相结合，利用贪心算法和推导的误差估计构建投影空间，同时自然地满足Hermite插值条件，从而大幅降低跨参数域评估输入输出关系的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性控制系统在参数域上的输入输出评估通常计算成本高昂，传统方法难以同时兼顾精度与效率，需要一种能够在用户指定精度下高效计算时间区间内系统输出的方法。

Method: 1) 提出轮廓积分方法（CIM），通过对修正积分轮廓应用求积规则近似逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 将CIM与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）结合，利用贪心算法构建投影空间；3) 为该设置推导误差估计用于指导贪心算法；4) 证明该投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件。

Result: 在基准非参数化和参数化控制系统上验证了该方法的准确性和效率，与最先进的基于投影的MOR方法对比表现更好，能显著降低参数域内输入输出关系的评估成本。

Conclusion: CIM结合投影MOR的方法为参数化线性控制系统的输出计算提供了一种高效且精确的方案，适用于广泛的输入函数和初始条件，有效提升了跨参数域的计算效率。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [168] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出异构多搜索搜索成本博弈的精确解：隐藏者将两个相同球分布在三个盒子中，搜索者可自适应开盒直至找到两个球。博弈值由三个显式有理函数的最大值给出，最优隐藏者策略为受限的乘积形式分布，所有非浪费的确定性搜索策略等价于72棵决策树之一，对应42种不同的开盒次数分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构多搜索（heterogeneous multi-look）搜索成本博弈的精确解。此前此类问题缺乏完整的解析结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将博弈转化为零和线性规划问题；利用三个参数区间分别分析最优隐藏者策略（乘积形式分布，分别限制在3、5或全部6种放置方式上）；将所有非浪费确定性搜索策略化简为72棵等价基本决策树（仅产生42种不同的开盒次数分布）；用解析论证处理第一个参数区间，用精确的Searcher混合策略分别处理第二（2个混合）和第三（4个混合）区间；通过精确有理Bernstein基证书验证参数化混合策略在全部成本区域上的可行性。

Result: 博弈值被精确刻画为三个显式有理函数的最大值。独立实现复现了策略集、零和线性规划值以及Bernstein验证器检查的全部154个二进节点，证明结果可靠。

Conclusion: 该问题被完全精确求解，为异构多搜索搜索成本博弈提供了完备的解析刻画，并展示了零和博弈与有理Bernstein证书相结合的分析方法在搜索博弈中的有效应用。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [169] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火场景下弱势群体（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的扶持疏散问题，提出了一个两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径，并严格满足时间窗约束。针对NP难复杂度，作者提出了基于逻辑型Benders分解的创新求解方法，包含组合性Benders割和逻辑不等式。通过在科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据上进行大量数值实验，证明该方法相比替代策略能显著提升避难所布局、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火情境下弱势群体（医院患者、长期护理居民）的扶持疏散问题至关重要但研究不足。这类疏散具有严格的时间窗约束和不确定性，且问题本质上是NP难的，需要高效、高质量的优化方法来解决设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径的联合决策问题。

Method: 建立两阶段随机优化模型，在第一阶段决策设施选址和车队规模，第二阶段在不确定性实现后决策车辆路径；采用基于逻辑型Benders分解（Logic-Based Benders Decomposition）的求解方法，引入组合性Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）和逻辑不等式来加速收敛，克服NP难复杂性。

Result: 基于科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据进行的广泛数值实验表明，所提出的方法能获得高质量解，在避难所选址、车辆利用率以及整体疏散效率等方面相比替代策略均有显著改进。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段随机优化框架与逻辑型Benders分解求解方法能够有效支持野火场景下弱势群体的疏散规划，为应急管理部门提供了可操作的决策工具，在提升疏散效率和资源利用率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [170] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DASSP算法，一种用于约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）计算的单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法。通过子空间分裂重构将Stiefel流形约束与二次约束解耦，首次为CDFT计算提供具有严格收敛保证的算法，在合成和真实问题上均表现出高可行性精度和良好效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT离散化优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构产生收敛问题。亟需一个能够同时保证约束精确性和收敛性的高效算法。

Method: 本文首先利用旋转不变性引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂重构，将两组约束解耦；基于此提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法DASSP。每次迭代包含谱最小化步、投影梯度步和阻尼对偶上升步，均具有高效实现。

Result: 在合成和实际CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP达到了高可行性精度，在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下展现出良好的效率，并且是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法。

Conclusion: DASSP为大规模CDFT应用提供了可靠高效的算法基础，有望推动CDFT在电子激发态和电荷局域态等物理化学现象研究中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [171] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（被积函数可能非光滑非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数之和。该框架仅要求被积函数满足一种非光滑、局部化的下降引理变体，无需正则项的（弱）凸性，也无需随机预言机方差的统一上界。在样本量序列仅需非递减且无界（无指定增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛及轨迹聚点平稳性；利用KL性质升级为全轨迹收敛，并对指数型KL函数和多项式增长的样本量给出显式多项式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机近端方法通常要求代价函数光滑或（弱）凸，且需要随机梯度的方差有界等严格假设，限制了许多实际应用。该工作旨在放宽这些条件，提出一个能同时处理非光滑、非凸被积函数以及非凸正则项的通用框架，并在更宽松的假设下获得收敛保证。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 每步用逐渐精细化的样本均值替代期望代价；(2) 采用Armijo型线搜索的近端次梯度步长选择，确保在随机误差下满足充分下降性质；(3) 利用KL（Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz）性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛；(4) 样本量序列仅要求非递减且无界，不预设增长率。

Result: 在样本量序列仅为非递减且无界的宽松条件下，证明函数值序列几乎必然收敛且轨迹每个聚点都是平稳点；借助KL性质建立全轨迹收敛到单一平稳点；对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长样本量，推导出函数值与迭代点的显式多项式收敛率（至多差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了近端随机次梯度方法所能处理的问题类别，即使在光滑情形下也提供了新的收敛保证。其假设更为一般（无需正则项的（弱）凸性、无需方差统一上界），理论上限更宽松但仍保持几乎必然收敛和明确的收敛率。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [172] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL+-TP），该方法采用两阶段自适应动量策略加速收敛，并在非负矩阵分解和非负CP分解等机器学习问题上优于多种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于地震前异常分析和机器学习等实际应用中，现有方法在收敛速度和参数选择方面存在局限性，需要更高效的求解算法。

Method: 提出了惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL+-TP），其核心创新包括：(1) 两阶段自适应动量策略动态更新外推参数；(2) 允许使用两个不同的外推点加速收敛；(3) 两个外推点的外推参数彼此独立且不受其他参数约束。同时证明了目标函数的单调收敛性、序列全局收敛到临界点并建立了收敛速率。

Result: 将该方法应用于稀疏非负矩阵分解（ℓ0约束）和稀疏非负CP分解（ℓ0约束）两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题，数值实验表明该方法在求解性能上优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: IBPL+-TP方法在保持理论保证（单调收敛、全局收敛到临界点、收敛速率）的同时，通过自适应动量策略和灵活的外推点设计显著提升了求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题的效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [173] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的迭代收敛框架，适用于广泛的Bregman近端方法（BPMs）和复合目标函数。通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质并引入依赖于核的参数化函数，证明了扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立，建立了离散时间BPMs的迭代收敛性。同时，基于参数化函数证明了镜像流（连续时间BPM）对o-minimal可定义目标函数收敛到驻点，这是首个无需凸性或孤点假设的镜像流轨迹收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法（BPMs）的迭代收敛性长期以来一直悬而未决，特别是对于非凸目标函数。已有的工作（chen2026skl）通过缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质取得进展，但仅适用于Shannon熵核和线性约束问题，缺乏统一性和通用性。

Method: 本文采用以下方法：(1) 扩展chen2026skl中SKŁ性质这一核心分析工具；(2) 引入依赖于核的参数化函数，证明扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立（特别是核具有闭域时）；(3) 验证标准BPMs在温和正则条件下满足框架假设；(4) 基于参数化函数分析连续时间BPM（镜像流）在o-minimal可定义目标函数下的轨迹收敛性。

Result: (1) 建立了适用于广泛核和复合目标函数的统一迭代收敛框架；(2) 证明了扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立；(3) 验证了标准BPMs在温和正则条件下的迭代收敛性；(4) 证明了镜像流对o-minimal可定义目标函数收敛到驻点，这是首个无需凸性假设或驻点隔离假设的镜像流轨迹收敛结果。

Conclusion: 离散时间和连续时间的收敛结果共同为BPMs提供了一个统一的轨迹收敛理论，大幅扩展了先前工作的适用范围，从Shannon熵核、线性约束问题推广到广泛的核类和复合目标函数，并在连续时间情形下首次消除了凸性和隔离性假设。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [174] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于重定心域的Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），通过物理信息神经网络（PINNs）离线条演FKE解快照，并利用主成分分析构建低维密度演化表示，再以轻量残差代理模型在线预测密度演化，实现高效的非线性目标跟踪。在几何约束跟踪实验中，RD-YYF相比扩展卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波具有更低的跟踪误差，同时保持高效的单步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器通过前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）传播概率密度实现非线性状态估计，但将其应用于目标跟踪时，需要在有限计算域上高效逼近FKE。因此，如何构建一种在有限计算域上高效求解FKE并保持跟踪精度的滤波器，是该研究的核心动机。

Method: RD-YYF将FKE在固定大小的局部窗口内求解，窗口以最新状态估计为中心进行重定心，从而将数值分辨率聚焦于主要后验密度区域。离线阶段，利用物理信息神经网络生成FKE解快照，通过主成分分析构建密度的低维演化表示，并训练一个轻量残差代理模型，将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数。在线阶段，利用预训练代理模型预测重定心窗口内的密度演化，随后进行观测更新和状态估计。

Result: 在两个具有几何约束的目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF），同时保持了高效的逐时间步推理。消融实验表明，域重定心改善了高概率区域的密度逼近精度，并加速了离线PINN的收敛。

Conclusion: RD-YYF展示了在非线性目标跟踪中兼顾精度与效率的潜力，为Yau-Yau滤波器在有限计算域上的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [175] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源软件包，实现了针对多阶段随机整数规划的可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法。除了标准割族（Benders、强化Benders、Lagrangian割）外，该算法还引入ReLU割以保证一般混合整数状态变量的收敛性。针对割生成中对偶问题多最优解导致的割强度差异问题，文章提出基于归一化和正则化的两种割选择策略，并结合交替割准则以平衡计算开销，在四类测试问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划求解困难，现有Benders分解算法对一般混合整数状态变量缺乏收敛保证。虽然ReLU割能提供收敛性，但其生成过程中对偶问题常存在多个最优解，不同解产生的割在逼近子问题成本函数的强度上差异显著，需要有效的割选择策略来提升算法性能。

Method: 该软件包实现了可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法，包含Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割和ReLU割等多类割族。针对ReLU割对偶问题的多重最优解问题，提出了两种割选择策略：基于归一化的策略和基于正则化的策略。此外，设计了交替割准则，在Benders割有效时优先使用低成本割，仅在必要时调用昂贵但更紧的割。算法在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准实例上进行了系统评估。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划实例上的计算实验对比了不同割选择策略的性能，揭示了问题结构对算法实际表现的影响，验证了归一化和正则化割选择策略以及交替割准则的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的开源软件包为多阶段随机整数规划提供了可证明收敛且实用的求解工具，归一化和正则化策略能有效增强割强度，交替割准则在计算效率与割紧度之间取得了良好平衡，问题结构显著影响各策略的适用性。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [176] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式问题的连续时间投影神经网络的收敛性分析。针对一类既非Hessian生成也非标量的状态相关矩阵度量，证明了投影在自变量和度量上的联合正则性，改进了欧几里得Lipschitz估计，并在多种条件下建立了局部和全局指数收敛结果，同时发现周期轨道环结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续时间投影神经网络收敛性分析仅覆盖两类逆度量：由Hessian生成的逆度量和状态相关的标量度量。对于更一般的矩阵度量（其逆不属于上述两类），缺乏系统的收敛性理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立更一般度量条件下的投影正则性和指数收敛保证，从而扩展投影神经网络的应用范围。

Method: 本文采用微分几何与动力系统分析相结合的方法：(1) 证明投影算子在自变量和度量参数上的联合正则性；(2) 利用谱界条件将欧几里得Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为线性界；(3) 对Lipschitz强单调算子和Lipschitz度量建立局部指数收敛（含显式速率与半径）；(4) 在紧可行集上利用显式度量变分界建立全局指数收敛；(5) 对二阶连续可微、一致正定度量及强单调线性算子，证明周期轨道环的存在性，并通过确定性数值计算验证解析公式。

Result: 主要结果包括：投影算子联合正则性定理；欧几里得Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为原界；Lipschitz强单调算子下的局部指数收敛（含显式速率和收敛半径）；紧可行集上的全局指数收敛；逆度量在整个环区域上违反Hessian可积性的周期轨道环。数值实验确认了解析公式并量化了两种收敛性证明中的余量。

Conclusion: 本文建立了更一般矩阵度量下投影神经网络的完整收敛性理论，证明了稳定和指数收敛无需Hessian结构或标量形式假设，揭示了新的动力学现象（周期轨道环），为投影神经网络的鲁棒设计提供了理论基础和量化工具。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [177] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆向优化方法，从专家的可观测决策中联合学习其偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，并通过三个案例研究（排班问题、生产计划问题和实际路径规划问题）验证了该方法在捕捉和复现专家决策方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家决策策略往往隐式且难以显式表达，学习并迁移专家知识对于培训新手、改进人机系统性能以及实现自主系统至关重要。由于专家的隐式偏好和决策规则难以直接获取，需要从可观测的决策数据中学习可解释的决策模型。

Method: 采用逆向优化（inverse optimization）方法，构建联合学习框架，同时估计决策者的偏好（感知成本）和支配其选择的决策规则。该方法通过观测数据反演优化模型中的目标函数参数和约束规则。

Result: 在三个案例研究中（排班分配问题、生产计划问题和真实路径规划问题），同时建模感知成本和决策规则相较于仅建模单一因素获得了更好的预测性能，验证了该框架在捕捉和复现专家决策方面的更大灵活性和有效性。

Conclusion: 联合学习专家偏好和决策规则的逆向优化框架具有广泛适用性，能够更灵活、更准确地捕捉专家的决策过程，为专家知识迁移和自主决策系统提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [178] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于Hilbert空间中可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法，通过将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸情形下的"类干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法可收敛到临界点或近似临界点，并在KL性质下建立全序列收敛性，同时可推广解决弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有惯性梯度算法在处理非凸优化问题时存在参数选取不灵活、收敛性分析受限等不足。作者希望通过将干摩擦性质从凸情形推广到非凸情形，并结合线搜索技术，设计一种更具适应性和通用性的快速梯度算法，同时保持理论收敛保证。

Method: 1) 将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为"类干摩擦"性质，适用于非凸设定；2) 采用线搜索技术在各迭代中自适应更新参数；3) 利用Moreau包络的变分性质，将算法扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题；4) 通过参数灵活性，算法可退化为多种现有的带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法。

Result: 算法实现了到临界点的子序列收敛或到近似临界点的全序列收敛；在代价函数的KL性质下建立了临界点的全序列收敛性；将凸KL函数的Moreau包络KL指数结果扩展到更广泛的非凸非连续KL函数类；仿真结果验证了算法效率及结合类干摩擦、外推和线搜索的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 该算法通过类干摩擦性质与线搜索技术的结合，为Hilbert空间中的非凸优化提供了一种快速且理论上可保证收敛的框架，同时具有参数灵活性以涵盖多种现有算法，并能处理弱凸非光滑问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [179] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究通过最小化Bregman损失来拟合线性回归模型，尽管生成势函数是强凸的，但回归目标在参数θ上可能非凸。论文将Hessian矩阵表示为加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差，并据此给出了局部强凸性、光滑性及梯度下降条件线性收敛的简单条件。针对二次-四次势函数，推导了精确的标量凸性条件，识别了负曲率区间，并通过数值实验表明标量条件可能过于保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统凸回归框架中，生成势函数的凸性与优化几何之间通常是一致的；然而在对偶Bregman回归中，即使生成势函数φ强凸，最终的回归目标在θ上仍可能非凸。这种凸性缺口使得梯度下降等优化算法的收敛性保证变得不明确，因此需要刻画残差依赖性曲率如何与生成势和设计矩阵交互作用。

Method: 论文首先将回归目标的Hessian重新表示为加权Gram矩阵，其中权重由势函数在预测点的二阶导数及当前残差决定。基于这一表示，推导了局部强凸性、C^2光滑性和梯度下降线性收敛的充分条件。随后针对二次-四次势函数φ(t)=t²+λt⁴，精确推导了标量凸性条件，确定了负曲率区间，并给出了局部与全局的正曲率充分条件，最后通过数值实验验证了分析结果。

Result: 针对二次-四次势函数得到了精确的标量凸性条件，识别出负曲率区间，并获得了正曲率的局部与全局充分条件。数值实验表明：即使标量条件不满足，完整Hessian在评估点仍可能是正定的（标量条件偏保守）；同时随四次项参数增大，可收敛的梯度下降步长范围缩小。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了逆向Bregman回归中残差依赖性曲率与生成势函数及设计矩阵的交互机制，为理解非凸Bregman回归的优化几何提供了理论工具，并为实际中选择步长和判断收敛性提供了指导性条件。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [180] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究度序列优化问题及其在二次函数情况下的复杂性，引入了图的临界根概念，并证明随着二次根的变化，问题最优值在临界根定义的区间上是凸分段仿射的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础组合优化问题，即给定一个图和关于顶点度的函数，寻找一个子图使得所有顶点上该函数值之和最大化。理解该问题在二次函数情形下的结构与复杂性，对图论和组合优化的理论和应用均有重要意义。

Method: 本文通过引入图的"临界根"概念，系统地刻画了当二次函数的根变化时，度序列优化问题最优值的行为特征。具体而言，临界根将参数空间划分为若干区间，在每个区间上最优值表现为凸的分段仿射函数。

Result: 证明了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的最优值——作为二次根的函数——在由图的临界根所定义的区间上是凸分段仿射的。这一结构性质为该问题的复杂性分析提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文为二次度序列优化问题建立了新的结构理论，通过临界根和凸分段仿射最优值刻画了问题的解空间结构，为后续深入分析该问题的计算复杂性奠定了基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [181] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定条件下最大化加权覆盖，并通过机场监控场景实验验证其优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中面临传感器位置不确定性问题，且不同监控区域具有不同的优先级权重，现有方法未能同时兼顾加权覆盖优化与位置扰动鲁棒性。

Method: 构建融合监控约束与RRF鲁棒性公式化的数学优化模型，并设计优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法来求解传感器方向，以最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场监控场景实验中，该框架能有效将感知资源导向高优先级区域，加权覆盖性能优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全敏感环境中具有实际应用价值，能够在位置不确定性下实现优先级感知的鲁棒覆盖优化。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [182] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层防御攻击者模型（BDAM），通过下层层对偶化将其攻击者路径项分解为单层混合整数规划，从而在18个基准实例上实现了精确最优性认证；同时指出供应规则在平局下不适定，并通过威胁走廊公式保持平局不变性；最后提出MILP-DA方法，在多数配置上优于此前混合元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层隔离（bi-level interdiction）模型通常只能由元启发式求解，而其解质量在测试规模下因缺乏精确最优解而无法评估。本文旨在为BDAM提供精确最优解，以验证和改进此前元启发式的求解质量。

Method: 1) 将BDAM的主导攻击者路径项通过下层面（lower-level）对偶化转化为精确的单层重构，得到混合整数规划；2) 提出威胁走廊（threat-corridor）公式以处理供应规则在平局下的不适定性；3) 设计MILP-DA方法，将精确锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: 在全部18个三行和五行配置上均能认证最优性，其中17个在10秒内完成；在15×30网格上有强非认证候选解。此前发布的平均值低于该前沿3.55%，且差距随规模增大而扩大。平局移动对目标影响小于10^-3，但对实现的供应成本影响最高达0.83单位。MILP-DA在13个认证配置和全部18个较大配置上获胜，所有失败均出现在三行网格上。

Conclusion: 精确重构方法能够为BDAM提供可认证的最优解，揭示了元启发式方法的实际误差随规模扩大而增大；供应规则中的平局缺陷在客观目标上被抑制但并未真正消除；MILP-DA成为更优的求解方案。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [183] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制问题，提出了一种新的谱分配方法，用于为无模型策略迭代（PI）获取初始稳定控制器，并通过累积因子调整和系统数据设计了不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代需要初始稳定控制器作为起点，但传统初始化依赖完整模型信息，限制了无模型PI的应用。对于具有状态和控制相关乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性由包含漂移和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式决定，而非确定性情形下的Hurwitz条件，因此无模型PI的初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种新颖的谱分配方法：利用Lyapunov型算子的谱，通过调整累积因子使原始系统逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近，从而获得稳定控制增益；进一步利用系统数据并调整累积因子，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法以实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出方法的有效性，表明谱分配方法和无模型算法能够成功获得稳定控制器并实现最优控制。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了连续时间不定随机LQ控制中无模型PI的初始化难题，提出了一种不依赖初始稳定策略且仅基于系统数据的模型无关算法，拓展了策略迭代在更复杂随机系统中的应用范围。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [184] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种考虑传输延迟影响的新分布式可诊断性条件，并通过延迟记录器结构和新的诊断函数进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式故障诊断研究通常假设代理之间的通信无延迟，但在实际应用中传输延迟是不可避免的。因此需要一种新方法来判断在存在传输延迟的情况下，系统是否仍能在有限步内诊断故障。

Method: 提出了扩展自集中式可诊断性条件的新分布式可诊断性条件，并构建了名为延迟记录器（delay recorder）的新颖结构以及新的诊断函数来验证该条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功地确定故障是否可被诊断。

Conclusion: 该工作为存在传输延迟的部分可观测离散事件系统提供了分布式故障可诊断性的判定方法，填补了现有分布式诊断框架对延迟鲁棒性分析的空白。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [185] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon（一种矩阵感知优化方法）在Stiefel流形上的扩展，发现Stiefel Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，并据此提出了Skewon算法——一种针对正交约束优化问题的高效实用方法，同时给出了光滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种近期提出的矩阵感知优化方法，但现有将其扩展到Stiefel流形（由正交列矩阵构成）的方法依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各异，缺乏精确且高效的解决方案。

Method: 作者证明了Stiefel流形上的Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，并利用该结果设计了Skewon算法，该算法具有高效实现，并建立了在光滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛性保证。

Result: 提出了Skewon算法，证明了其在光滑非凸环境下的一阶收敛性，提供了比现有启发式/近似方法更精确且高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: Stiefel Muon更新可以在闭式形式下精确求解，基于此提出的Skewon算法为正交约束优化提供了兼具理论保证和实际效率的新方法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [186] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数z_{wL}(m,3)在m=5,6,7,8,9时的取值，均为2m，并由此推出BSR(m,3)≥2m对m=5到9成立，强有力地支持了z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对一切m≥5的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数z_{wL}(m,3)的精确值，验证其满足z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5的猜想。由于BSR与z_{wL}之间存在下界关系，该结论可直接导出BSR(m,3)的下界。

Method: 下界通过显式的弱容许构造（weak admissible constructions）给出；上界（对m=7,8,9）通过对规范的无C_4基图进行精确有限搜索获得。

Result: 确定了z_{wL}(5,3)=10, z_{wL}(6,3)=12, z_{wL}(7,3)=14, z_{wL}(8,3)=16, z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对5≤m≤9成立；同时推出BSR(m,3)≥2m对m=5,6,7,8,9成立。

Conclusion: 结合已知小情形，这些结果有力支持了z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的猜想。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [187] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的收敛性，重点关注同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）在理论性质上的差异，揭示了S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数的高度敏感性，并证明了在某些条件下S-SEG不收敛的负结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机外梯度方法的研究主要集中于独立样本SEG（I-SEG），且假设要么定义域紧致、要么随机算子方差一致有界；而同样本SEG（S-SEG）虽然是与I-SEG性质截然不同的自然变体，但研究极少。此外，大多数分析依赖强假设，对无界域和放松假设下的情况缺乏理解，因此需要填补这些理论空白。

Method: 作者首先构造反例证明S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数的敏感性——仅依赖均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以保证其在紧集上收敛。随后，对于可能无界的定义域，在放松的假设集下为两种SEG变体建立了高概率受限间隙（restricted-gap）收敛结果，并证明了某些基本改进在一般情况下不可能实现。最后，通过构造具体的随机单调VIP反例，证明已知能够保证I-SEG几乎必然最后迭代收敛的非对称双步长选择在S-SEG下会失效。

Result: (1) 证明了S-SEG在仅有均值Lipschitz性和有界方差假设下即使在紧集上也可能发散；(2) 在放松假设下为I-SEG和S-SEG建立了高概率受限间隙收敛，并给出相应的下界表明结果的最优性；(3) 构造了一个随机单调VIP，在该问题上S-SEG在修改步长下几乎必然发散，建立了S-SEG与I-SEG在非对称双步长策略下的根本差异。

Conclusion: S-SEG与I-SEG在理论性质上存在本质差异：S-SEG需要更强的假设（如样本级Lipschitz参数）才能保证收敛，且某些对I-SEG有效的算法设计（如非对称双步长）并不能推广到S-SEG。这些发现为随机外梯度方法在变分不等式中的应用提供了更精确的理论指导。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [188] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从边缘概率密度函数观测中推断广义常微分方程组动力学参数的框架。该方法利用增广状态空间中联合密度满足的Liouville方程，将随机或不可逆过程重铸为确定性可逆流，通过特征线表示避免求解高维Liouville方程，并采用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器进行随机梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际系统中的随机/不可逆动力学（如扩散过程）难以直接从低维状态观测中推断。传统方法通常需要处理高维密度演化方程或复杂的随机微分方程推断，计算代价高且数值不稳定。作者希望将这类问题转化为确定性ODE的参数恢复问题，从而简化推断过程。

Method: (1) 将低维观测的随机/不可逆过程重铸为增广状态空间中满足Liouville方程的确定性可逆流，其中潜变量承载随机性与记忆；(2) 利用特征线表示（characteristic representation）避免直接求解高维Liouville方程，粒子沿特征线从初始分布传播；(3) 沿特征线传播参数灵敏度（Eulerian sensitivity），通过交叉U统计量（crossed U-statistic）构造无偏梯度估计器；(4) 采用随机梯度下降法优化参数。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法有效性：三模式线性系统（单模式边缘观测）、带隐藏模式的非线性Gompertz增长模型、隐藏模式使单峰边缘分布变为双峰的双稳态系统、以及细胞流中Stokes--Oseen阻力定律恢复。文中还分析了各设定下的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将随机/不可逆动力学推断转化为确定性ODE的参数恢复问题，通过特征线表示和交叉U统计量梯度估计器实现了高效且无偏的推断，在多种动力学系统中均展现出良好的收敛性和恢复精度。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [189] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界域上多维连续性方程（含源项）通过局部内通量反馈的镇定问题，证明在有限时间特征阻尼不等式等几何条件下可实现L2指数稳定，并给出了输入到状态的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维连续性方程在有界域上的镇定问题尚未被充分解决，尤其是通过局部化内通量反馈的方式。本文旨在建立一套系统的几何与增益条件，保证误差动力学在L2意义下指数稳定，并推广到非线性速度场情形。

Method: 该方法通过散度形式的局部内通量反馈构造控制输入，使得关于参考剖面的误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程。核心几何条件是有限时间特征阻尼不等式，要求相关特征线在时间窗口内积累均匀的阻尼量；结合与压缩放大相关的中继增益条件，以及Lyapunov型入口条件和加权Lyapunov泛函，分别推导出指数稳定性和微分Lyapunov判据，并进一步给出ISS估计。此外还讨论了椭圆右逆实现和非线性速度场的推广。

Result: 在有限时间特征阻尼不等式与增益条件成立的前提下，误差在L2中指数稳定；加权Lyapunov泛函提供了微分Lyapunov判据及关于加性扰动的输入到状态（ISS）估计；椭圆右逆实现被用于构造反馈通量，且论证可推广至非线性速度场；二维实例验证了几何、增益与实现条件的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多维连续性方程通过局部内通量反馈实现镇定提供了完整的理论框架，给出了明确的几何条件、增益条件和实现途径，并展示了向非线性情形的可扩展性，对输运方程控制领域具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [190] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散事件系统中观测延迟场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了两种考虑延迟观测的诊断与预测条件，并通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数来验证这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际系统中，观测信息可能因通信或传感器原因存在延迟，而现有的故障诊断与预测方法大多假设观测是即时可得的。因此需要针对延迟观测场景重新审视诊断性（diagnosability）和可预测性（prognosability）的概念，现有的可预测性定义过于保守，需要修正。

Method: 提出了两种适用于延迟观测场景的诊断与预测条件；引入延迟观测器（delay observer）来构建系统的延迟状态估计；设计了一个新的验证函数（verification function）用于检验所提出的诊断性与可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够有效地验证延迟观测条件下系统的故障诊断与预测性质，而且修正后的可预测性定义避免了原有定义带来的保守性。

Conclusion: 延迟观测器与验证函数的组合方法为离散事件系统中带观测延迟的故障诊断与预测提供了有效的验证框架，修正后的可预测性概念相比现有方法更具精确性和非保守性。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [191] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究电力系统规划中综合考虑稳定性措施（惯量与电压稳定约束）的集成方法，发现相比传统顺序规划方法，集成方法可降低系统成本，且电网构网型电池储能系统（grid-forming BESS）的双重用途选项优于静态同步补偿器等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源（IBR）在电力系统中渗透率不断提高，传统同步发电机对动态电压稳定性和惯量响应的贡献需要被替代。然而，现有的电力系统规划方法通常是顺序式的，即稳定措施仅在规划过程的后期阶段才被考虑，这可能导致次优的规划和成本效率低下的结果。

Method: 提出一种集成式电力系统规划方法，在发电扩张、输电和储能系统规划的同时，通过简化的惯量和电压稳定性约束，将稳定措施同步纳入优化考虑，并与传统的顺序规划方法进行对比分析。

Result: 集成规划方法能够降低系统成本；电网构网型电池储能系统（grid-forming BESS）凭借其双重用途（既提供稳定支持又参与能量调度）优于静态同步补偿器（STATCOM）等其他稳定措施。

Conclusion: 集成式规划方法相比顺序规划更具经济优势，且构网型电池储能系统的双重用途特性使其成为最具吸引力的稳定措施选择，为高比例逆变器资源电力系统的规划提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>
